Abstract: Persian (Farsi) is often described as a low-resource language in natural language processing, but that label collapses distinct shortages into a single category. This paper argues that Persian is more precisely described as annotation-scarce, provided that the term is understood as a property of its NLP resource ecology rather than an intrinsic property of the language. The review covers 34 representative Persian text resources available by July 2026 and adds three quantitative cross-checks. First, independent web measurements place Persian among roughly the twenty most visible content languages: W3Techs reports Persian on about 0.9% of websites with a known content language, while Common Crawl CC-MAIN-2026-30 identifies Persian as the primary language of 0.7039% of HTML pages. Second, a selective speech review shows a long resource trajectory from FARSDAT to recent corpora containing hundreds or thousands of hours of speech. Third, a matched Persian-English comparison normalizes task-specific annotation volumes by relative Common Crawl web presence. The resulting ratios vary sharply: Persian syntax and news NER are comparatively dense, whereas natural-language inference falls below the web-proportional baseline. The evidence therefore does not support a simple claim that Persian is globally deficient in labeled volume. Instead, annotation scarcity is expressed through uneven task and domain coverage, incompatible schemes, access and documentation friction, and limited supervision for specialist domains, preference data, and varieties beyond standard Iranian Persian.

</details>


### [141] [Lost in Speech: Trilingual Spoken Hallucination Detection Across Audio and Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24707)
*Meruyert Aristombayeva,Jason S. Lucas,Chaewan Chun,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个多语言口语幻觉检测基准，包含12,013条英语、俄语和哈萨克语新闻样本，并评估了不同检测方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本幻觉检测研究较多，但口语幻觉检测，特别是针对低资源语言的，仍然研究不足。

Method: 构建包含三类三种严重程度的受控幻觉的多语言口语幻觉基准，结合真实假新闻，评估微调多语言编码器和多模态解码器在转录本与直接音频处理上的性能。

Result: 转录本检测通常优于直接音频处理；合成训练检测器在真实假新闻上迁移效果良好；俄语来源分析揭示真实性和模型相关的机器特征信号。

Conclusion: 转录本检测方法更有效；合成幻觉基准中存在需要量化的重要混淆因素。

Abstract: While text-based hallucination detection has been extensively studied, spoken hallucination detection remains largely unexplored, particularly for low-resource languages. We present the first multilingual spoken hallucination benchmark comprising 12,013 news samples across English, Russian, and Kazakh with controlled hallucinations of three types and three severity levels. Samples comprise original articles and aligned hallucinated counterparts in text and audio. We complement the synthetic corpus with 290 fact-checked fake news items collected natively in Russian (225) and Kazakh (65), translated into the other language and rendered through the same TTS-ASR pipeline. We assess fine-tuned multilingual encoders and, in zero-shot in-context settings, multimodal decoder models on transcript-based versus direct audio processing. Transcript-based detection generally outperforms direct audio processing, with binary-task degradation for strong encoders tracking per-language ASR error. On real-world fakes, synthetic-trained detectors transfer strongly (macro-F1 0.82-0.88 on original text), while Russian provenance analysis reveals both veracity-related and model-dependent machine-style signals, quantifying a key confound in synthetic hallucination benchmarks.

</details>


### [142] [Arbitrary Polygon Oscillator: Generalizing Polygonal Synthesis to Arbitrary Shapes, Morphing, and Three-Dimensional Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24726)
*Antonio Argentieri,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用的多边形合成系统，采用恒定弧长速度（而非恒定角速度），支持任意顶点配置的多边形作为波形发生器，实现了不同顶点数多边形间的平滑形态变换，并将该范式扩展到三维多面体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅使用恒定角速度，且限于规则的参数化定义多边形。作者希望突破这些限制，支持任意闭合多边形（规则、不规则或星形），实现不同多边形之间的平滑过渡，并将多边形合成扩展到三维空间。

Method: 1) 将振荡器改造为可从外部缓冲区接受任意顶点数据；2) 设计混合插值算法实现不同顶点数多边形间的平滑形态变换；3) 将范式扩展到三维——通过固定水平面切割绕三轴旋转的凸多面体，得到连续可变的多边形截面；4) 在RNBO平台实现，采用几何缓存策略避免每样本重新计算；5) 结合基于运行时贝塞尔切线导出的四点polyBLAMP抗混叠校正与自适应过采样。

Result: 构建了一个统一的多边形合成引擎，能够处理任意闭合多边形（规则、不规则、星形），支持无参数化描述的中间形态多边形插值，并将合成范式成功扩展到三维多面体。系统高效运行于RNBO平台，无需每样本重新计算几何数据。

Conclusion: 本文成功将多边形合成从规则参数化形状推广到任意顶点配置，实现了不同多边形类型间的连续形态变换，并拓展到三维领域，同时通过几何缓存和自适应抗混叠保持了实时性能。这一统一框架为声音合成开辟了更丰富的形状空间。

Abstract: Polygonal synthesis generates audio by traversing the perimeter of a polygon with a phasor; prior work uses a constant angular velocity, whereas the proposed system adopts constant arc-length (perimeter) velocity. Existing formulations operate on regular, parametrically defined polygons, producing smooth timbral transitions within a single family of shapes.
  This paper generalizes polygonal synthesis around a unified arc-length engine: vertex data of any origin feed the same DSP pipeline. First, we adapt the oscillator to accept arbitrary vertex configurations from an external buffer, opening the possibility for a broad class of closed polygons -- regular, irregular, or star-shaped -- to function as a waveform generator. Second, a hybrid interpolation algorithm enables smooth morphing between polygons with unequal vertex counts, passing through intermediate shapes that have no parametric description. Third, we extend the paradigm to three dimensions: a convex polyhedron rotated about three axes is sliced by a fixed horizontal plane, and the resulting cross-section yields a continuously variable polygon controlled by the solid's orientation. The system runs in RNBO (Cycling~'74) with a geometry caching strategy that avoids per-sample recomputation. Antialiasing combines a four-point polyBLAMP correction derived from runtime Bézier tangents with adaptive oversampling, adapting the correction geometrically to general vertex configurations without per-shape analytical derivation.

</details>


### [143] [SkillForge: Evolving Verifiable Skills for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24747)
*Shidong Yang,Ziyu Ma,Tongwen Huang,Xucong Wang,Renda Li,Yiming Hu,Yong Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SkillForge框架，通过持续验证和精炼技能，解决LLM智能体在强化学习中无法跨任务积累可复用知识的问题，显著提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体通常采用强化学习训练，但多数模型仅具备阶段性学习能力，无法跨任务积累和复用知识。现有基于技能的方法（如SkillRL）虽尝试提取技能，但将技能库视为只追加不验证的存储库，无法确保存储技能的有效性。

Method: 提出SkillForge框架，实现技能的持续演化。核心创新包括：1）在智能体交互过程中明确技能使用，使强化学习可直接优化环境动作和技能调用决策；2）引入基于证据的技能验证机制；3）采用多路径技能归纳方法，使技能库在持续扩展的同时保持质量。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和AppWorld三个任务上进行了广泛实验，结果显示SkillForge持续优于SkillRL基线方法。

Conclusion: 持续验证的技能能够有效训练出更强的LLM智能体，SkillForge框架通过技能的持续验证与精炼，显著提升了智能体在复杂决策任务中的表现。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are trained with reinforcement learning (RL) for complex decision-making tasks. However, most RL-trained agents remain episodic and cannot accumulate reusable knowledge across episodes. Recent skill-based approaches, such as SkillRL, attempt to address this issue by extracting skills from raw trajectories, but treat the skill bank as an append-only repository without verifying whether stored skills remain effective. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a framework for continuous skill evolution that enables skills to be verified and refined through environment interaction. By making skill usage explicit during agent interaction, RL can directly optimize both environment actions and skill invocation decisions. SkillForge further introduces evidence-based skill verification and multi-pathway skill induction, allowing the skill bank to continuously grow while maintaining its quality. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and AppWorld show that SkillForge consistently outperforms SkillRL, demonstrating the effectiveness of continuously verified skills in training stronger LLM agents.

</details>


### [144] [The RAT: A Unified Bayesian Model for RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24753)
*Pius von Däniken,Felix Matthias Saaro,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯评估框架，联合建模RAG系统的检索成功率、拒绝行为与答案正确性，通过条件分解揭示看似等价系统间的行为差异，并解决标注分配问题，集成LLM-as-a-judge评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统评估需超越端到端正确性，关注组件交互与错误传播机制；现有边际指标无法区分系统行为差异；人类标注成本高昂，需整合自动化评估以降低成本。

Method: 构建贝叶斯评估框架，根据信息流因子分解，联合建模检索成功、拒绝行为和答案正确性；区分任务成功（生成器输出正确答案）与策略遵守（生成器基于检索结果的适当行为）；分析标注分配问题，提供信息论解释；扩展模型以整合LLM-as-a-judge作为校准噪声观测。

Result: 在27种RAG配置（三个数据集、三个检索器、三个生成器）上验证，条件分解揭示系统间显著行为差异；证明检索成功标注比任务成功标注更具信息量；实现有限人类判断与自动化评估的统一概率模型整合。

Conclusion: 框架为RAG评估提供更细粒度、可解释的评估方法，揭示边际指标掩盖的行为差异；指导评估资源有效分配，促进人类与自动化评估的协同使用，提升RAG系统评估的全面性和成本效益。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems requires assessing not only end-to-end correctness but also how individual components interact and how errors propagate through the pipeline. We introduce a Bayesian evaluation framework that jointly models retrieval success, abstention behavior, and answer correctness, factorized according to the pipeline's information flow. The model distinguishes task success. Whether the user received a correct answer (from generator success) and whether the generator behaved appropriately given the retrieval outcome. We apply the framework to 27 RAG configurations across three datasets, three retrievers, and three generators, and show that the conditional decomposition reveals substantial behavioral differences between systems that appear equivalent under marginal metrics. We further analyze the annotation allocation problem, demonstrating that retrieval-success annotations are more informative than task-success annotations for estimating policy adherence, and provide an information-theoretic explanation for this asymmetry. Finally, we extend the model to incorporate LLM-as-a-judge annotations as calibrated noisy observations, enabling practitioners to combine limited human judgments with cheaper automated assessments within a unified probabilistic model.

</details>


### [145] [ExpConCAD: Experience-Guided Text-to-CAD Generation from Shape Descriptions with Implicit Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24760)
*Jingyao Liu,Jinkang Tang,Chen Huang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出ExpConCAD框架，通过恢复构建结构、检索相似经验约束来补全文本描述中缺失的空间约束，并生成可执行的CAD程序。实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到CAD任务旨在从自然语言描述生成可执行CAD程序，但现实世界描述往往存在信息不足，遗漏有效的空间约束，这一挑战在现有方法中被普遍忽视。

Method: ExpConCAD是一个经验增强的隐式空间约束补全框架，其工作流程包括：1)恢复预期的构建结构和约束范围；2)检索相似范围的约束补全经验来补全缺失的空间约束；3)生成可执行的CadQuery程序。该方法主张缺失的空间约束应根据底层构建结构进行推断，并利用可复用的设计经验来指导。

Result: 大量实验证明了ExpConCAD的有效性，并提供了关于构建结构理解和经验记忆在空间约束补全中作用的见解。代码已公开在GitHub上。

Conclusion: 通过引入构建结构理解和经验记忆机制，ExpConCAD有效解决了文本描述中空间约束不足的问题，为文本到CAD任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD aims to generate executable CAD programs from natural-language descriptions. However, real-world descriptions are often underspecified and omit critical spatial constraints required for valid CAD construction, a challenge that has been largely overlooked by existing methods. In this paper, we argue that missing spatial constraints should be inferred with respect to the underlying construction structure and informed by reusable design experience. Based on this insight, we propose ExpConCAD, an experience-enhanced framework for implicit spatial constraint completion. ExpConCAD first recovers the intended construction structure and constraint scopes, then retrieves relevant constraint-completion experience for similar scopes to complete the missing spatial constraints, and finally generates executable CadQuery programs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of ExpConCAD and provide insights into the role of construction structure understanding and experience memory in spatial constraint completion. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hotjiashell/ExpConCAD.

</details>


### [146] [Linear Probing Provides Robust and Efficient Detection of Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24780)
*Gerrit Quaremba,Hanqi Yan,Elizabeth Black,Denny Vrandecic,Elena Simperl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器生成文本与人类文本在潜在空间中的线性可分性，提出基于简单线性探针的检测方法，该方法在跨域检测任务中显著优于现有基线，仅需少量样本即可达到接近峰值性能，并能够捕捉文本"机器化"程度的连续谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于机器生成文本的潜在滥用风险，区分机器生成文本与人类文本日益重要。然而现有监督式检测器在域外场景性能下降显著，且需要大规模多样化训练集。

Method: 通过系统分析机器生成文本与人类文本潜在表示的线性性与质量差异，训练两种变体的线性探针，在四个基准数据集上与16个基线方法进行对比评估。

Result: 线性探针在域外检测任务中表现一致提升（AUC+11），仅需少于100个样本即可达到接近峰值性能。探针通过恢复跨场景通用的共享潜在机器生成方向实现迁移能力，并能捕捉"机器化"程度的连续谱，为精细估计AI编辑文本提供潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了机器生成文本与人类文本在潜在空间中的本质差异，证明了线性探针作为鲁棒且样本高效检测方法的潜力，并开源了相关代码。

Abstract: Distinguishing machine-generated text (MGT) from human-written text (HWT) becomes increasingly important due to potential misuse. However, most supervised detectors often degrade out-of-domain (OOD) and require large, diverse training sets. In this work, we analyze the linearity and quality of MGT representations and show that simple linear probes outperform a wide range of detectors while being substantially more sample-efficient. We first show that MGT and HWT latent representations are linearly separable in low-dimensional space, and provide a plausible explanation for this separability through systematic differences in their representation quality. Motivated by these insights, we train two variants of simple linear probes and evaluate them across 4 benchmarks against 16 baselines. Probes consistently improve OOD detection (+11 AUC), requiring solely ${<}100$ samples to reach near-peak performance. We show that this transferability arises because probes recover a shared latent MGT direction that generalizes across diverse settings. Finally, we demonstrate that probing vectors capture a continuous spectrum of ``machineness'', highlighting their potential for fine-grained estimation of AI-edited text. Overall, our work provides insights into latent-space differences between MGT and HWT and demonstrates the potential of linear probes as as robust and sample-efficient MGT detectors. We release our code on~\href{https://github.com/gerritq/mgt_probes}{github}.

</details>


### [147] [Structurally-bounded Agentic Graph Exploration for Evidence-Grounded Scholarly DeepSearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24809)
*Rima Hazra,Sayan Layek,Somnath Banerjee,Soumen Chakrabarti,Animesh Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Crase，一种用于学术检索的有界且可检查的替代方案，相比深度研究代理具有更优性能与更低成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究代理存在搜索过程无界、计算成本高昂、决策逻辑不透明等问题，难以满足学术研究中对可控性和效率的需求

Method: 通过单次搜索引擎查询获取种子论文，扩展其1.5跳引用邻域，基于语义蕴含关系剪枝无效引用边，采用时间感知随机游走对剩余论文排序，所有候选集、保留理由及终止条件均在推理前显式定义

Result: 在LitSearch及50万篇arXiv语料基准上，Crase在recall@50指标上相较专有模型构建的深度研究代理提升高达3倍，同时成本降低约三分之二

Conclusion: Crase通过可解释的有界搜索流程，在保持高召回率的同时显著降低计算开销，为学术检索提供了更高效、透明的技术路径

Abstract: We present Crase, a bounded and inspectable alternative to deep research agents for scholarly search. Instead of an open-ended search loop, Crase queries a search engine once for seed papers, expands them along their 1.5-hop citation neighborhood, prunes citation edges whose claims lack entailment support, and ranks the remaining papers with a recency-aware random walk. This makes the candidate set, the reason each paper is kept, and the stopping condition explicit and fixed before inference. On LitSearch and one further benchmarks over a 500K-paper arXiv corpus, Crase outperforms deep research agents built on proprietary models by up to 3$\times$ recall@50 at roughly a third of the cost.

</details>


### [148] [Reading Is Not Using: Retrieval, Judgment, and the Design of AI Financial Research Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24842)
*Miao Liu,Zhizhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在长上下文金融分析中存在"检索-整合差距"：即使能准确检索风险信息，该信息对投资决策的影响也会随无关上下文增加而衰减至噪声水平。AI分析师性能由模型能力和工作流架构共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型日益被部署为AI分析师处理金融披露并支持投资决策，但现有评估仅关注检索能力，未验证检索到的信息是否真正影响判断。研究旨在揭示检索与整合之间的差距，探究其成因和解决方案，为构建可靠的AI金融分析系统提供指导。

Method: 设计控制实验，固定目标公司信息，仅改变无关上下文长度（2,000至128,000 tokens），观察风险信息对投资判断的影响。实验覆盖多个模型家族和判断任务，并验证实际10-K文件中的披露信息。采用因果记忆干预，测试压缩摘要和源文本查找的联合作用。对比不同工作流架构（分块-摘要流水线 vs. 决策附近结构化重述）对信息传递的影响。

Result: 发现风险信息对投资判断的影响随无关上下文增加而下降至实验噪声水平，同时直接检索保持准确。该模式在多个模型家族和判断任务中重现。更强大的模型仅延缓但未消除差距。因果干预表明压缩摘要和源文本查找共同传递披露信息至判断。工作流架构决定传递成败：分块-摘要流水线会剔除相关信息，而决策附近结构化重述可恢复其影响。

Conclusion: AI分析师性能由模型能力和工作流架构共同决定。基于检索的评估可能认证那些投资决策忽略其实际检索信息的系统。因此，评估LLM金融分析能力需同时考虑检索准确性和信息整合有效性，优化工作流架构至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as AI analysts to process financial disclosures and support AI-assisted investment decisions. Yet such systems are usually evaluated by what they can retrieve, not whether retrieved information affects their judgments. We identify a retrieval-integration gap in long-context financial analysis. Holding focal-firm information fixed and varying only unrelated context from 2,000 to 128,000 tokens, we find that a risk disclosure's influence on investment judgments falls to the experimental noise floor even as direct retrieval remains accurate. The pattern replicates across model families and judgment tasks and in experiments removing real disclosures from actual 10-K filings. More capable models postpone but do not eliminate the gap. Causal memory interventions show that compressed summaries and source-text lookup jointly transmit disclosures into judgments. Workflow architecture determines whether this transmission succeeds: chunk-and-summarize pipelines evict relevant information, whereas a targeted, structured restatement adjacent to the decision restores its influence. AI analyst performance is therefore jointly determined by model capability and workflow architecture. Retrieval-based evaluations can certify systems whose investment judgments ignore information they demonstrably retrieved.

</details>


### [149] [BrowserForge: Scaling Web Episode via Parallel Browser Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24848)
*Fei Tang,Huawen Shen,Zhiqiong Lu,Zhengxi Lu,Pengyuan Lyu,Chengquan Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BrowserForge通过并行驱动数十万公开网站上的浏览器沙箱，利用提议-求解双智能体循环生成大规模高质量网页交互轨迹数据集，用于训练仅基于截图的网页智能体，显著提升了在线任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于像素的网页智能体避免了HTML/无障碍树的脆弱性和高token成本，但其训练依赖大规模高质量交互轨迹，而现有数据集规模小、网站覆盖有限，自动化合成流程也受限于预定义网站列表，缺乏可大规模扩展的数据生成方法。

Method: BrowserForge框架包含三个组件：1) 开放网络来源阶段：探索数十万真实可达网站；2) 沙箱集群管理器：高利用率调度数百并发浏览器；3) 提议-求解双智能体循环：将原始页面转化为可执行任务并收集验证轨迹。采用规则+模型清洗流水线去除失败运行并统一推理链。训练智能体仅使用截图，页面结构（如无障碍树）仅作为合成信号。

Result: 生成203,238条轨迹，每条来自不同网站，数据集规模与多样性超过先前轨迹数据集。在该数据上微调紧凑型多模态模型，将在线Online-Mind2Web成功率从25.66%提升至33.33%，并持续提高静态Multimodal-Mind2Web的步骤准确率，且增益随数据规模增长。控制实验证实开放网络来源和广泛网站覆盖是关键改进因素。

Conclusion: BrowserForge有效解决了网页交互数据大规模生成的开放问题，通过开放网络来源和广泛网站覆盖，为像素级网页智能体提供了大规模、多样化的训练数据，显著提升其在线任务性能。

Abstract: Web agents that act from rendered pixels avoid the fragility and heavy token cost of reading a page's HTML or accessibility tree, but training them depends on large amounts of high-quality interaction trajectories, and how to produce such data at scale remains an open problem. Public datasets typically contain only a few thousand trajectories drawn from a fixed and narrow set of websites, and even recent automated synthesis pipelines stay bound to predefined site lists or tutorial sources, so the number of distinct websites the agent ever sees barely grows. We present BrowserForge, a framework that generates web interaction data at scale by driving many browser sandboxes in parallel over the open web. BrowserForge couples three components: an open-web sourcing stage that exposes the agent to hundreds of thousands of real, openly reachable websites; a sandbox cluster manager that schedules hundreds of concurrent browsers with high utilization; and a Proposer-Solver dual-agent loop that turns a raw page into an executable task and then collects a verified trajectory for it. A rule-plus-model cleaning pipeline removes failed runs and rewrites the surviving reasoning into a single unified chain-of-thought style. Page structure such as the accessibility tree is used only as a synthesis-time signal; the agent we train and release acts purely from the screenshot. The resulting corpus contains 203,238 trajectories, each collected from a distinct website, larger and more diverse than prior trajectory datasets. Fine-tuning a compact multimodal model on this corpus raises its success rate on the live Online-Mind2Web from 25.66% to 33.33% and consistently improves step accuracy on the static Multimodal-Mind2Web, with the gain growing as the corpus scales. Controlled analyses further confirm that open-web sourcing and broad website coverage are key contributors to the observed improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Pattern-Derived Visual Swarm Games: Multi-Scale Drone-Vision States for Interception and Sustainability Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23575)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将无人机视觉标注流转换为无需控制实体无人机的虚拟集群博弈状态，通过Bloom压缩、确定性探测、有限零和博弈及扩展马尔可夫层，实现屏幕安全、编码精度与目标定位性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在不直接操控物理无人机的情况下，高效利用无人机视觉与集群元数据，提升虚拟集群博弈的鲁棒性、安全性与定位精度。

Method: 将VisDrone与UAVSwarm元数据压缩为Bloom表示；通过确定性探测生成能力向量、图像空间阵型与有限零和收益，并附加可读可视化层。审计规模从6×6扩展至32×32有限博弈，并引入含库存、疲劳、适应、暴露、压力、预算、数据增长、模型改进与熵预算状态变量的重复马尔可夫层。采用本地屏幕调优与有限经验风险编码器（基于尺度归一化高斯带宽，λ=1.50）提升屏幕安全与编码精度。服务端审计遍历16,777,216个目标定位状态及32轮重复博弈轨迹，筛选预算自适应策略。

Result: 本地屏幕调优将鲁棒屏幕安全从0.526提升至0.593，32×32调优屏幕价值达0.616。字段读出审计显示固定像素栅格精度非单调，128×128精度67.2%，热点误差0.136，诊断误差源于图像平面带宽缩小。经验风险编码器在128×128下精度提升至77.6%，联合损失降低0.185。服务端审计选出价值0.461的预算自适应策略。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将无人机视觉标注流转换为可审计的虚拟集群博弈状态，通过多维状态扩展与自适应编码显著提升安全与精度，为无人机集群仿真与视觉感知提供了一种可扩展、可验证的框架。

Abstract: We convert drone-vision annotation streams into virtual swarm-game states without controlling physical drones. VisDrone and UAVSwarm metadata are compressed into a Bloom representation; deterministic probes produce bounded capability vectors, image-space formations, finite zero-sum payoffs, and human-readable visual overlays. The audit scales from $6\times 6$ to $32\times 32$ finite games and adds a repeated Markov layer with stock, fatigue, adaptation, exposure, stress, budget, data-growth, model-improvement, and entropy-budget state variables. Local screen tuning raises robust screen security from $0.526$ to $0.593$, and the $32\times 32$ tuned screen reaches value $0.616$. A field readout audit shows that fixed-pixel rasters do not improve monotonically: $128\times 128$ accuracy is $67.2\%$ and hotspot error is $0.136$. The diagnosed error is shrinking image-plane bandwidth. A finite empirical-risk encoder over scale-normalized Gaussian bandwidths selects a scale-normalized encoder with $λ=1.50$, reaching $77.6\%$ accuracy at $128\times 128$ and reducing joint loss by $0.185$. A server-side audit checks $16{,}777{,}216$ target-localization states, and a 32-round repeated-game audit over $16{,}777{,}216$ trajectories selects a budget-adaptive policy with value $0.461$.

</details>


### [151] [Macro-Operator Generation and Predicate Selection for TAMP Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23629)
*Can Emir Bora,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过自动发现因果关联动作对生成宏操作符的TAMP系统，并结合谓词剪枝优化符号状态，显著加速规划并提升长序列任务的可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工创建符号操作符是TAMP系统部署的主要瓶颈。现有基于演示数据学习操作符的方法通常孤立学习各动作，无法捕获操任务的多步重复结构，导致长序列任务搜索变得难以处理。此外，符号状态中每个谓词在每个搜索节点都被评估，即使该谓词从未出现在任何学习到的操作符中，造成冗余计算。

Method: 系统核心组件是宏操作符自动生成：从训练数据中直接发现因果关联的动作对（前一个动作恰好产生后一个动作所需条件），将每对转化为新操作符，压缩重复动作序列为单步规划。同时，系统剪枝未被任何学习操作符引用的谓词，缩小每个搜索节点评估的符号状态。

Result: 在四个TAMP领域上，该方法相比基线方法（Learning Operators for TAMP）实现最高4.6倍的规划加速。更重要的是，它解决了基线方法无法解决的长序列任务，表明宏操作符发现在某些领域不仅加速规划，还决定了实际可解性。

Conclusion: 宏操作符自动发现结合谓词剪枝有效缩短了规划视界，收益随任务长度增长而增加。该方法不仅提升规划效率，在复杂领域中还使原本不可解的长序列任务成为可能。

Abstract: Creating symbolic operators by hand is one of the main bottlenecks in deploying Task and Motion Planning systems (TAMP). Recent works show that these operators can instead be learned directly from demonstration data. Existing methods, however, typically learn each action in isolation and cannot capture the recurring multi-step structure of manipulation tasks, so the search becomes intractable on long sequential tasks. A further inefficiency arises in the symbolic state: every provided predicate is evaluated at every search node, even when it never appears in any learned operator. We present a system that addresses both problems together. Its central component is the automatic generation of macro-operators, composite actions that compress a recurring sequence of individual actions into a single planning step. Our system discovers causally linked action pairs directly from the training data, where one action produces exactly the condition that the next one requires, and turns each pair into a new operator. Alongside this, our system prunes every predicate that no learned operator references, which shrinks the symbolic state evaluated at each search node. Together, these changes shorten the effective planning horizon, and the benefit they bring grows with the length of the task. Across four TAMP domains, our method reaches up to a 4.6x planning speedup compared to the baseline method, namely Learning Operators for TAMP. More importantly, it solves a long sequential task that the baseline cannot solve. Macro-operator discovery thus not only accelerates planning but, in certain domains, determines solvability in practice.

</details>


### [152] [Concept-Guided Exploration: Building Persistent, Actionable Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23650)
*Noé Zapata,Gerardo Pérez,Alejandro Torrejón,Pedro Núñez,Pablo Bustos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种概念优先的机器人空间感知架构，通过异步概念代理直接实例化管理语义实体（房间和门），构建可操作的、人类可解释的空间表示，无需依赖预存的全局度量地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人的3D空间感知正从扁平度量网格表示向混合度量-语义图转变。大多数方法先构建度量地图再添加语义层，本研究探索替代的概念优先架构，使空间理解从异步概念代理中涌现，直接实例化和管理语义实体。

Method: 机器人采用两种空间概念（房间和门），在认知分布式架构中实现为自主过程。概念代理通过主动探索和增量验证协作构建室内布局的共享场景图表示。关键架构原则是层次约束传播：房间实例化提供几何和语义先验，指导和支持门在墙壁边界内的检测。结构维护基于预测-匹配循环的互补功能原则。

Result: 该方法产生了可操作的、人类可解释的空间表示，不依赖任何预存的全局度量地图，支持可扩展操作和在结构化室内环境中的持久性、任务相关理解。

Conclusion: 概念优先架构通过层次约束传播和预测-匹配循环，成功实现了从语义概念到空间表示的直接构建，为移动机器人在室内环境中提供了有效的空间感知和理解框架。

Abstract: The perception of 3D space by mobile robots is rapidly moving from flat metric grid representations to hybrid metric-semantic graphs built from human-interpretable concepts. While most approaches first build metric maps and then add semantic layers, we explore an alternative, concept-first architecture in which spatial understanding emerges from asynchronous concept agents that directly instantiate and manage semantic entities. Our robot employs two spatial concepts (room and door), implemented as autonomous processes within a cognitive distributed architecture. These concept agents cooperatively build a shared scene graph representation of indoor layouts through active exploration and incremental validation. The key architectural principle is hierarchical constraint propagation: Room instantiation provides geometric and semantic priors to guide and support door detection within wall boundaries. The resulting structure is maintained by a complementary functional principle based on prediction-matching loops. This approach is designed to yield an actionable, human-interpretable spatial representation without relying on any pre-existing global metric map, supporting scalable operation and persistent, task-relevant understanding in structured indoor environments.

</details>


### [153] [Learning to Act While Waiting: RL Finetuning of Generalist Robot Policies Under Inference Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23831)
*Brian Zhu,Momen Khalil,E Harrison,Emanuele Poggi,Philipp Schmitt,Bernd Kast,Philine Meister,Pranav Atreya,Qiyang Li,Finn Ferchau,Cesar Colmenero,Yash Shahapurkar,Gokul Narayanan,Melih Erdogan,Kai Wurm,Georg von Wichert,Oier Mees,Eugen Solowjow,Andrew Wagenmaker,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARLI框架通过异步推理与状态增强机制，解决了大型通用策略（如VLA）在推理延迟下强化学习失效的问题，实现了延迟环境中的有效策略微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代通用机器人策略（如VLA）模型规模庞大导致推理延迟严重，引发环境动态改变并破坏马尔可夫假设，使标准强化学习算法完全失效。

Method: 基于异步推理方法，通过状态增强（纳入已提交动作和推理中间观测）恢复近马尔可夫结构，并设计低延迟RL策略以最大化推理窗口内的响应能力。

Result: 在模拟和真实操作任务中评估表明，该方法在推理延迟下实现了标准RL无法完成的有效微调，性能匹配甚至超过无延迟理想设置下的标准RL表现。

Conclusion: ARLI框架成功克服了大型通用策略推理延迟对强化学习的阻碍，为部署中的持续策略改进提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) allows generalist robot policies to continually improve during deployment, the large model size of modern generalist policies, such as VLAs, poses a fundamental obstacle to effective RL improvement. In particular, their severe inference latency---which can lead to pauses or jerky movements---can alter the effective environment dynamics and, if not correctly accounted for, break the Markov assumption that RL relies on, causing standard RL algorithms to fail completely. In this work, we introduce a latency-aware framework, Asynchronous RL with Intermediate Information (ARLI), that enables RL-based improvement of generalist policies under inference delays.
  Our framework builds on asynchronous inference approaches, which interleave action generation with execution to hide latency, and addresses its incompatibility with RL by providing a low-latency RL policy design that maximizes reactivity within the inference window through two contributions: state augmentations that restore near-Markovian structure by incorporating committed actions and a mid-inference observation.
  We evaluate our approach across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, and find that it enables effective finetuning under inference delays where standard RL fails entirely, even matching or exceeding the performance of standard RL in idealized no-latency settings.

</details>


### [154] [Resilience Matters for Embodied Agents System: New Metrics, Systematic Evaluation, and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23839)
*Yapeng Liu,Yuanzhao Zhai,Xudong Gong,Dawei Feng,Bo Ding,Lin Wang,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 摘要：本文提出针对具身智能体系统（EAS）的韧性评估框架，克服传统结果导向指标的不足，揭示EAS在扰动下的恢复、稳定与扩展能力，通过实验验证了评估框架的诊断与优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：现有EAS评估依赖结果中心指标（如成功率），忽略了动态执行过程中的韧性，而韧性在开放世界环境中对部署质量和人机信任至关重要。

Method: 方法：借鉴韧性工程概念，提出可灵活应用于任意EAS的新型韧性评估框架，定义了涵盖反弹、稳定性与优雅扩展性的综合韧性指标套件，并实现了将执行过程转化为评估的诊断与优化层。

Result: 结果：在400个家庭任务和10个EAS上，揭示了结果指标隐藏的过程级差异，包括成功 episode 间的恢复成本差异（ΔC_rec=25.2）、不稳定性增加和任务族退化。基于指标的优化降低了恢复成本，提高了稳定性和优雅扩展性完成率。

Conclusion: 结论：韧性评估具有诊断效果，揭示了韧性特征间的权衡，建议根据部署特定需求配置韧性EAS。

Abstract: Embodied Agents System (EAS) are increasingly deployed in open-world physical domains, where reliability directly dictates deployment quality and human-agent trust. However, existing evaluations rely on outcome-centric metrics as success rate or safety scores that collapse diverse execution trajectories into coarse scores, obscuring the dynamic processes underlying agent behavior. Therefore, they ignore a critical property of EAS -- which we define as the Resilience -- that reflects how EASs recover, stabilize, and extend under perturbations and across iterative updates. The lack of resilience is particularly critical in open-world environments due to continuous unexpected disruptions, thus directly affecting the quality of EAS deployment. To address this problem, we gain insight from the resilience-engineering concepts to EAS groundings and propose a novel resilience evaluation framework that can be flexibly applied to any EAS. Specifically, we define the first comprehensive resilience metrics suite for EASs system that exposes Rebound, Stability, and Graceful Extensibility across embodied tasks execution, providing a practical grounding for EAS resilience analysis. We further implement the resilience evaluation layer that transforms execution process into assessments for diagnosis and optimization. Across 400 household tasks with 10 EAS, we reveal the process-level distinction hidden by outcome metrics, including recovery cost differences among successful episodes ($ΔC_{rec}=25.2$), increased instability and task-family degradation. Metrics-guided optimizations reduce recovery cost and increase stability, graceful extensibility completion, showing the diagnostic effect of resilience evaluation. Our results reveal a trade-off among resilience characteristics, suggesting that a resilient EAS construction should be configured according to deployment-specific requirements.

</details>


### [155] [DreamLedger: Execution-Settled Credit Files for World-Model Imagination in Robot Decision Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23863)
*Xianyao Li,Ruitong Tian,Rui Min,Fang Xu,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamLedger将机器人预测的可靠性建模为持久化部署对象，通过执行结算信用文件跟踪预测兑现率，实现可审计的预测消费管理，显著降低无效预测消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人依赖世界模型预测行动，但可靠性仅通过瞬时的、模型内部信号表达，缺乏持久化、可审计的可靠性跟踪机制，导致预测消费缺乏有效风险管控。

Method: 构建执行结算信用文件，按操作条件、区域和预测时域索引；将每次预测消费登记为索赔，通过归因阶段排除测量污染结果，零标注成本地结算到达的现实结果；低信用预测缩短依赖时域或触发额外观测；所有依赖事件通过依赖票据和可重放日志实现审计。

Result: 在三个模拟域（室内飞行、桌面操作、2D导航）及真实Franka机械臂上评估，信用门控规划将烧损想象（后期无法兑现的预测）减少62%（95% CI 43-81%），成功率与碰撞率相当；在匹配风险目标下，验证探测从1.00降至0.36/episode，成功率0.94 vs 0.98；在真实感知和接触噪声下保持运作，所有1062次消费均可从审计日志重放。

Conclusion: DreamLedger提供了一种跨解码器、潜空间和token空间接口的统一信任层，将瞬时可靠性信号转化为持久化、可审计的信用系统，显著提升预测消费效率同时维持任务成功率，适用于真实机器人部署。

Abstract: Robots are beginning to act on world-model predictions, yet reliability is still expressed through instantaneous, model-internal signals. DreamLedger instead treats reliability as a persistent deployment object: an execution-settled credit file recording how often consumed predictions are borne out, indexed by operating condition, region, and prediction horizon, and consulted before each use. Each consumed prediction is registered as a claim; attributable outcomes are settled against arriving reality at zero labeling cost, an attribution stage excludes measurement-contaminated outcomes, and a settlement-supervised head complements sparse bins. The resulting credit gates consumption: low-credit predictions shorten the dependent horizon or trigger additional observation; every reliance event remains auditable via dependency tickets and replayable logs. We evaluate DreamLedger in three simulated domains (indoor flight, tabletop manipulation, 2D navigation), via mounts on unmodified DreamerV3, TD-MPC2, and V-JEPA 2-AC, and on a real Franka manipulator. Claim failure is dose-monotone in all 12 held-out condition-horizon cells. Credit-gated planning reduces burned imagination (consumed claims that later fail to redeem) by 62% (95% CI 43-81%) versus blind consumption, with equal success and comparable collision rates. At matched risk targets, persistent books cut verification probes from 1.00 to 0.36/episode in manipulation, at success 0.94 versus 0.98; settlement-grounded calibration retains moderate, seed-consistent operating points unlike raw instantaneous gates. The same trust layer operates across decoder-, latent-, and token-space interfaces, including V-JEPA 2-AC settled on real robot frames. On hardware, settlement remains operational under real sensing and contact noise, a deployment failure loop is re-priced online, and all 1,062 registered spends replay from the audit logs.

</details>


### [156] [Interpreting Control Latents for System Identification via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23887)
*Dingqi Zhang,Ruiqi Zhang,Mark W. Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法将自适应策略中的隐变量解码为物理模型分布，实现了对固定自适应策略的调优、鲁棒性分析和诊断。在四旋翼飞行器上验证，在扰动执行器动力学下，预测性调优相比固定增益将位置跟踪RMSE降低23%，航向RMSE降低45%；在高斯力扰动下，解码模型集合能够准确预测横向跟踪误差演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐变量条件自适应策略虽然能控制具有变化动态的机器人，但学习的隐变量仅是策略的内部表示，而非可检查、可推演或可被其他控制模块使用的物理模型。这限制了对固定策略的闭环分析、诊断和进一步改进。直接将隐变量映射到物理参数存在欠确定性问题，因为多个系统可能产生相似的闭环行为。

Method: 采用条件流匹配技术，将每个操作隐变量解码为四旋翼飞行器模型分布。解码后的分布支持两种下游应用：(1)围绕固定低层策略的高层控制器在线预测性调优；(2)在指定扰动下的鲁棒性分析。该方法无需修改原始策略即可实现。

Result: 1. 在扰动执行器动力学条件下，解码模型预测性调优相比固定增益，位置跟踪RMSE降低23%，航向RMSE降低45%。2. 在高斯力扰动条件下，解码模型集合能够紧密预测横向跟踪误差的演化过程。实验验证了在四旋翼飞行器系统上的有效性。

Conclusion: 控制隐变量可以转换为物理模型集合，用于固定自适应策略的调优、鲁棒性分析和诊断。该方法突破了隐变量仅作为内部表示的局限，提供了可检查、可推演的物理模型，为自适应控制系统的分析、诊断和改进提供了新途径。

Abstract: Latent-conditioned adaptive policies can control robots across changing dynamics, but their learned latents remain internal representations of the policy rather than physical models that can be inspected, rolled out, or used by other control modules. This limits closed-loop analysis, diagnosis, and further improvement of a fixed policy. A direct mapping from latent to physical parameters is also under-specified, because multiple systems can induce similar closed-loop behavior. We therefore decode each operational latent into a distribution of quadrotor models using conditional flow matching. The decoded distribution enables two downstream uses without modifying the policy: online predictive tuning of a high-level controller around the fixed low-level policy, and robustness analysis under specified disturbances. Under perturbed actuator dynamics, decoded-model predictive tuning reduces position tracking RMSE by $23\%$ and heading RMSE by $45\%$ relative to fixed gains. Under Gaussian force disturbances, decoded-model ensembles closely predict the lateral tracking-error evolution. Together, these results show that control latents can be converted into physical model ensembles for tuning, robustness analysis, and diagnosis of frozen adaptive policies.

</details>


### [157] [Safety-aware Model Predictive Path Integral Control with Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23972)
*Yiqi Zhao,Taekyung Kim,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出safety-aware-stl-mppi框架，通过将信号时序逻辑(STL)约束编码为时间变化的控制障碍函数(CBF)并集成到模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制器中，实现计算高效的安全感知运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域的感知安全运动规划仍然面临挑战，特别是在时间紧迫的任务和复杂规格要求下，需要在保证安全性的同时保持计算效率。

Method: 将离散时间STL公式编码为候选时间变化控制障碍函数(CBF)，集成到模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制器中，形成基于采样的移动视界规划框架，利用CBF处理STL表达的约束。

Result: 在四个具有不同环境和成本设置的火星车规划案例研究中，与多个MPPI基线方法对比，展示了持续的高安全性和高效性。此外，在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中展示了四旋翼飞行器规划实验。

Conclusion: 该方法结合了可高效并行化的采样规划器的低计算成本优势与STL约束处理能力，在多样化规划场景中实现了高安全性和效率的平衡，为安全关键型机器人任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Safety-aware motion planning remains a challenge in robotics, especially when missions are time-critical and are under complex specifications. In this paper, we propose safety-aware-stl-mppi, a computationally efficient sampling-based receding-horizon planning framework designed to promote satisfaction of constraints expressed in Signal Temporal Logic (STL). Our approach encodes discrete-time STL formulas into candidate time-varying control barrier functions (CBF), which are integrated into a model predictive path integral (MPPI) controller. Our method inherits the benefits of low computational cost from an efficiently parallelizable sampling based planner and utilizes CBF for constraints expressed in STL. We compare against several MPPI baselines using four artificial Mars Rover planning case studies with a diverse environment and cost setups, where we show our method consistently achieving high safety and efficiency. We show a quadcopter planning experiment with NVIDIA Isaac Lab.

</details>


### [158] [Sensorless damage-safe grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23983)
*Yusei Shuto,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无额外传感器的应变限制抓取控制策略，在仿真与实验中均实现了高抓取成功率与低损伤率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不同成熟度水果刚度差异大导致固定握力难以兼顾“抓得牢”与“不压伤”的痛点。

Method: 仅利用伺服电机的编码器位置和扭矩信号，计算接触力并除以刚度下界来估计压缩应变，当应变达到预设阈值 $\varepsilon$ 时停止，该阈值保证了真实压缩不会超标。

Result: MuJoCo 仿真中，在中等至硬刚度范围内 100% 无损伤且抓取率超 98%；TPU 立方体实验中，同等成功率下握力减半，软物损伤率从 100% 降至 40%。

Conclusion: 基于应变阈值的控制优于固定力阈值，提供了可解释、随尺寸缩放的安全抓取参数，适用于学习型抓取策略。

Abstract: Robotic fruit harvesting must hold produce securely without bruising it, yet compression stiffness varies several-fold with ripeness within a single species, so no fixed grip force spans the range. Rather than tune force, we bound deformation: a controller closes the gripper until the object's estimated compression strain reaches a user-specified limit $\varepsilon$, using only the encoder position and motor-effort signal on every servo gripper---no tactile or force-torque sensor. Dividing an effort-based contact force by a lower bound on object stiffness makes the stop provably conservative---true compression stays at or below $\varepsilon$---for any $\varepsilon$ above a contact-detection strain floor we identify and quantify: robust detection itself spends compression, linearly in closing speed, making speed an explicit throughput--gentleness knob. Unlike a hand-tuned force threshold, $\varepsilon$ is a certified, size-scaling, operator-interpretable damage limit, and a ready safe-action parameter for learned grasping policies. In MuJoCo simulation over a realistic fruit-stiffness range, under a sensor-noise model calibrated to the real servo, the controller holds $\ge 98\,\%$ grasp at $0\,\%$ damage across all medium-to-firm stiffnesses for the entire certified $\varepsilon$ range, which neither fixed-force baseline attains; on stiffness-graded 3D-printed TPU cubes it matches baseline grasp success at roughly half the grip force and cuts soft-object damage from $100\,\%$ to $40\,\%$.

</details>


### [159] [Bridging Teacher Expectations and Robot Learning via Coupling Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23994)
*Evan Dallas,Sean Dallas,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Human-robot teaching focuses on enabling nontechnical experts to customize robots according to their needs after deployment. With recent advances in machine learning, human-robot teaching is no longer confined to offline learning where the data gathering step from a human teacher is separated from when the robot learns. Instead, more recent approaches for human-robot teaching focus on coupling human teaching with robot learning. This coupling impacts the structure, timing, and content of the teaching and learning interaction. However, it is currently unclear how such coupling dynamics affect humanrobot teaching effectiveness and human perceptions towards the teaching process. Informed by human learning theories, in this paper we propose a new scale for classifying human-robot teaching interactions according to coupling dynamics present between the human teacher and robot learner. We apply this scale to a subset of the human-robot teaching literature to identify how coupling dynamics and human teacher mental model mismatches with the ground truth robot learning system affect teaching effectiveness and human perceptions towards the teaching process

</details>


### [160] [NeurRAFT: Robot Motion Planning via Anchor-Level Flow Matching with Clearance-Aware Preference Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24026)
*Sibo Tian,Chang Liu,Minghui Zheng,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeurRAFT提出基于锚点流匹配的生成式规划框架，通过雅可比加权损失训练和直接偏好优化(DPO)提升障碍物规避能力，在 cluttered 环境中实现高效安全的路径规划，显著优于现有方法并实现真实机器人零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端神经运动规划器虽避免了传统规划所需的几何模型，但在复杂环境中难以保证无碰撞规划。模仿学习无法区分安全轨迹与近碰撞轨迹，导致测试时仍出现碰撞风险行为。需要一种能直接优化安全性且无需增加推理成本的新方法。

Method: 1. 锚点级流匹配：使用紧凑锚点代替密集航点序列，减少冗余局部细节计算
2. 雅可比加权损失：训练时考虑每个锚点对任务空间的影响
3. 两阶段推理：先生成锚点再经三次样条插值恢复高分辨率轨迹
4. 直接偏好优化(DPO)：重塑预训练规划器的概率分布，将质量向大障碍间距轨迹转移，避免后处理修正

Result: 实验显示在 cluttered 环境中显著超越最先进规划器；Franka机器人实物实验证明在噪声深度观测和部分遮挡条件下可实现零样本迁移。

Conclusion: NeurRAFT通过锚点级表示和偏好优化有效解决了神经规划器的碰撞风险问题，在保证计算效率的同时大幅提升安全性，展现了在真实机器人系统中的实用价值。其DPO机制为神经规划器的安全优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent end-to-end neural motion planners generate trajectories from raw sensor observations, avoiding the privileged geometric models required by classical planners. However, collision-free planning in cluttered environments remains challenging. We present NeurRAFT, a generative planning framework based on anchor-level flow matching and clearance-aware preference tuning. Unlike prior neural planners that model dense waypoint sequences and spend capacity on redundant local details and smoothness, NeurRAFT operates on compact anchor waypoints. We train the planner using a Jacobian-weighted loss that accounts for the task-space impact of each anchor. At inference, the anchors are generated in two integration steps, followed by cubic-spline interpolation to recover a smooth, full-resolution trajectory. Since imitation learning from positive demonstrations cannot distinguish collision-free from near-collision trajectories, collision-prone behaviors persist at test time. Rather than relying on post-hoc corrections, we directly reshape the pretrained planner's distribution toward safer solutions without augmenting inference. Specifically, Direct Preference Optimization shifts probability mass toward trajectories with larger obstacle clearance, with the resulting improvement directly absorbed into the planner parameters. Experiments show substantial improvements over state-of-the-art planners, while real-world experiments demonstrate zero-shot transfer to a Franka robot under noisy and partially occluded depth observations. Video results available at https://neurraft.github.io/.

</details>


### [161] [Design-to-Plan: A Large Language Model-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Manufacturing Process Planning from 3D CAD Models and 2D Engineering Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24039)
*Muhammad Tayyab Khan,Lequn Chen,Wenhe Feng,Seung Ki Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出Design-to-Plan，一种基于LLM的多智能体框架，实现从设计表示到制造计划的全流程自动化，通过结构化协调连接设计信息与制造知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法聚焦于特征识别、图纸解释或工具选择等孤立子任务，难以支持从设计工件到工艺计划的全链路推理。当规划需要解释3D CAD模型、2D工程图纸、材料和领域特定规则时，这一局限性尤为关键。

Method: 采用编排器协调多个专业化智能体（3D特征识别、2D图纸分析、2D-3D上下文融合、知识检索、工艺排序、工具选择和报告生成）。混合架构中，确定性模块和专业智能体从CAD和图纸输入中提取结构化信息，而LLM智能体执行上下文感知推理、检索制造规则、解决冲突并生成计划输出。

Result: 在300个基准案例上评估：下游智能体成功率100%，工具F1分数95.9%-97.6%，冲突分析中源检测准确率90%，关键规划任务token使用量减少60%-68%。

Conclusion: 结构化LLM多智能体协调能够桥接设计表示与制造知识，实现可扩展、高效且可追溯的设计到计划自动化。

Abstract: Manufacturing process planning transforms heterogeneous design information into coherent manufacturing decisions. However, existing approaches focus on isolated subtasks, such as feature recognition, drawing interpretation, or tool selection, and struggle to support the full reasoning chain from design artifacts to process plans. This is critical when planning must interpret 3D CAD models, 2D engineering drawings, materials, and domain-specific rules. To address this gap, this paper presents Design-to-Plan, a large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent framework for end-to-end manufacturing process planning. An orchestrator coordinates specialized agents for 3D feature recognition, 2D drawing analysis, 2D-3D context fusion, knowledge retrieval, process sequencing, tool selection, and report generation. Rather than using LLMs as standalone text generators, the framework deploys them as reasoning agents that interact with deterministic modules and knowledge sources to produce consistent and traceable decisions. In this hybrid design, deterministic modules and specialized agents extract structured information from CAD and drawing inputs, while LLM agents perform context-aware reasoning, retrieve manufacturing rules, resolve conflicts, and generate planning outputs. The framework is evaluated using 300 benchmark cases across three downstream ReAct-enabled agents, plus separate evaluations of CAD feature recognition, drawing analysis, and 2D-3D context fusion. The parallel architecture achieves 100% success across downstream agents, Tool F1 scores of 95.9%-97.6%, 90% source detection accuracy in conflict analysis, and a 60%-68% reduction in token usage for key planning tasks. Results show that structured LLM-based multi-agent coordination can bridge design representations and manufacturing knowledge, enabling scalable, efficient, and traceable design-to-plan automation.

</details>


### [162] [Hierarchical Skill Retrieval for Data-Efficient Adaptation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24042)
*Haoran Hao,Shahram Najam Syed,Jeff Schneider,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化技能检索(HSR)框架，通过分解目标任务为技能序列并进行混合检索，实现基于少量示教的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型高效适应，在LIBERO基准和真实机器人任务上分别提升成功率10.3%和21.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人数据集预训练的VLA模型在新任务适应时性能下降；现有检索方法依赖视觉相似性、状态-动作表示或任务级语言匹配，忽略了长视界操纵任务的层次结构，完整任务匹配罕见但可复用技能丰富。

Method: HSR框架包含三个核心步骤：(1)将目标任务分解为候选技能序列，基于语义合理性和技能可靠性评估；(2)混合检索结合子任务级语言检索和行为特征重排序；(3)两阶段预训练和微调管道，分离通用技能获取与任务特定适应。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中平均成功率提升10.3%，在多个真实世界机器人操纵任务中提升21.3%，均超过最强基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构化技能级检索能有效提升VLA模型的数据高效适应能力，证明了层次化技能分解对于机器人任务适应的重要性。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pretrained on large-scale robot datasets provide a strong foundation for robot manipulation, their performance can degrade when adapted to new tasks with limited task-specific demonstrations. Retrieval offers a practical way to reuse existing demonstrations for data-efficient adaptation, but existing methods often rely on visual similarity, state-action representations, or task-level language matching. These approaches may overlook the hierarchical structure of long-horizon manipulation tasks, where complete task matches are rare but reusable skills are often abundant. To address this challenge, we propose Hierarchical Skill Retrieval (HSR), a retrieval framework for data-efficient VLA adaptation. Specifically, HSR first decomposes a target task into candidate skill sequences. It evaluates each plan based on both semantic plausibility and skill reliability estimated from the prior dataset. The selected decomposition is then used for hybrid retrieval. This combines subtask-level language retrieval with behavior-feature reranking to identify demonstrations that are both semantically relevant and compatible with the target task. Finally, we adapt the policy through a two-stage pretraining and finetuning pipeline, which separates general skill acquisition from task-specific adaptation. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark and several real-world robot manipulation tasks show that HSR improves the average success rate by 10.3% and 21.3% over the strongest baseline, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of structured skill-level retrieval for data-efficient VLA adaptation. Videos and code are available at https://hoar012.github.io/HSR-Project.

</details>


### [163] [SIREN-Bench: Behavior-Driven Generation and Evaluation of Emergency-Vehicle Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24094)
*Yicheng Zhu,Tianmu Zhao,Haoxin Leng,Fan Zuo,Tao Li,Zilin Bian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIREN行为驱动协同仿真平台，解决紧急车辆-民用车辆交互评估中缺乏行为级控制与一致感知数据的问题；通过SUMO-CARLA耦合实现L1-L3级紧急交互模拟，验证显示轨迹预测最难且现有模型平均不如恒速参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集和仿真基准无法同时提供紧急车辆特权行为控制、民用车辆响应行为控制、一致感知及真值标注，难以评估安全关键的紧急车辆-民用车辆交互行为。

Method: 构建SIREN平台，耦合SUMO的网络级交通演化/行为逻辑与CARLA的连续车辆控制/同步车载感知；根据激活行为由SUMO、CARLA或二者联合控制交互；实例化为SIREN-Bench-v1，包含7种参数化交互模板（覆盖L1-L3紧急等级和3类行为族），附带同步传感器观测和仿真器原生标注。

Result: 在3D目标检测、轨迹预测和视觉语言风险理解任务中：1) 交通清空交互对检测最难；2) 特权路口通行对预测最难；3) 9种轨迹预测器平均表现劣于恒速参考；4) 视觉语言模型在近失/碰撞事件中显著劣于正常交通场景。

Conclusion: 验证了以行为为中心的基准测试价值，SIREN作为可扩展的数据生成与评估平台，对自动驾驶和交通安全研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Emergency vehicles (EMVs) can reorganize surrounding traffic as civilian vehicles brake, change lanes, or form rescue corridors in response to their passage. Evaluating these safety-critical interactions requires behavior-level control over both EMV privileges and civilian responses, together with consistent sensing and ground truth. Existing datasets and simulation benchmarks do not directly provide this combination. We present \textbf{SIREN}, a behavior-driven SUMO--CARLA co-simulation platform for generating EMV--civilian interactions. SIREN couples SUMO's network-level traffic evolution and behavior logic with CARLA's continuous vehicle control and synchronized onboard sensing; depending on the active behavior, the interaction is controlled by SUMO, CARLA, or jointly. We instantiate the platform as \textbf{SIREN-Bench-v1}, comprising seven parameterized interaction templates across emergency levels L1--L3 and three behavior families, with synchronized sensor observations and simulator-native annotations. We demonstrate the benchmark through three representative tasks: 3D object detection, trajectory prediction, and vision-language risk understanding. Evaluations of nine trajectory predictors, four LiDAR-based detectors, and five vision-language models reveal behavior-dependent failure modes. Traffic-clearance interactions are hardest for detection, privileged intersection traversal is hardest for prediction, and no learned predictor outperforms the constant-velocity reference on average. Vision-language models perform substantially better on normal traffic than on near-miss and collision events. These results demonstrate the value of behavior-centered benchmarking and establish SIREN as an extensible data-generation and evaluation platform for autonomous-driving and transportation safety research.

</details>


### [164] [TrAct: Bridging Robot Control and Visual Prediction with Visual Tracks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24101)
*Zhi Cao,Howard Ji,Kevin Zhang,Kuangzhi Ge,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Huang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrAct是一个基于世界模型的机器人决策框架，使用视觉轨迹作为控制与预测间的中间接口。该框架包含VLAT（联合预测动作与视觉轨迹）、TWM（轨迹条件世界模型）和VLAC（视觉语言奖励模型）三个组件，通过生成候选动作-轨迹对、预测视觉后果并选择最优轨迹来实现机器人决策。实验表明TrAct在仿真和真实世界中均显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作具有本体特定性，与图像空间视觉变化仅弱对齐，限制了其作为世界模型条件信号的有效性。相比之下，视觉轨迹提供了与本体无关的表示，能够描述任务相关点在场景中的移动方式，为准确和空间精确的未来视频预测提供密集图像空间指导。

Method: TrAct框架包含三个核心组件：(1) VLAT模型：从当前观察和语言指令联合预测候选动作和相应视觉轨迹；(2) TWM：基于提出的轨迹预测未来视觉结果；(3) VLAC：对预测结果进行评分。推理时，VLAT生成候选动作-轨迹对，TWM展开其视觉后果，VLAC选择最能满足指令的轨迹预测结果，执行与所选轨迹配对的动作。

Result: 在提出的LIBERO-INTEGRAL基准测试上，TrAct将成功率从27%提升至55%；在真实世界Franka操作任务中，成功率从49%提升至76%，均相比强VLA基线π₀.₅有显著改善。此外，TWM持续改善了视频预测质量，优于动作条件世界模型(AWM)。

Conclusion: 视觉轨迹提供了机器人控制和视觉预测之间的有效共享接口，实现了更准确的世界建模和更强的机器人泛化能力。TrAct框架通过引入视觉轨迹作为中间表示，成功克服了传统动作条件信号的局限性，为机器人决策提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Robot actions are inherently embodiment-specific and only weakly aligned with image-space visual changes, limiting their effectiveness as conditioning signals for robot world models. In contrast, visual tracks provide an embodiment-agnostic representation of how task-relevant points move through a scene, offering dense image-space guidance for accurate and spatially precise future video prediction. Building on this observation, we propose TrAct, a world-model-based robot decision-making framework that uses visual tracks as an intermediate interface between control and prediction. TrAct consists of three components: a Vision-Language-Action-and-Track model (VLAT) that jointly predicts candidate actions and corresponding visual tracks from the current observation and language instruction; a track-conditioned world model (TWM) that predicts future visual outcomes conditioned on the proposed tracks; and a vision-language reward model (VLAC) that scores the predicted outcomes. At inference time, VLAT generates candidate action-track pairs, TWM rolls out their visual consequences, and VLAC selects the track whose predicted outcome best satisfies the instruction; the action paired with the selected track is then executed by the robot. Experiments on the proposed LIBERO-INTEGRAL benchmark and real-world Franka manipulation show that TrAct improves success rates from 27% to 55% in simulation and from 49% to 76% on real-world tasks compared with the strong VLA baseline $π_{0.5}$. Furthermore, TWM consistently improves video prediction quality over the action-conditioned world model (AWM). These results demonstrate that visual tracks provide an effective shared interface between robot control and visual prediction, enabling more accurate world modeling and stronger robot generalization.

</details>


### [165] [Trajectory-Level Continuous Action Representation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24111)
*Tong Yang,Jingkai Jia,Yuecheng Xu,Xueyao Chen,Chi Zhang,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CAT框架，将动作轨迹编码为连续潜在令牌，通过频率感知位置编码实现不同控制频率下的时序一致性，在多种机器人操作任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动系统通常将动作表示与控制频率纠缠在一起，或依赖固定时间参数化，导致高采样率下表示冗余并限制关键运动建模。

Method: CAT将固定实时间隔内的动作轨迹编码为连续潜在令牌集合。采用频率感知位置编码建立共享时序坐标系，确保不同控制频率下的时序一致性。轨迹级正则化进一步稳定潜在表示。该方法防止表示随时间步密度增长，避免依赖预定义时间参数化。

Result: 在LIBERO、MimicGen和真实世界长程操作任务上进行了广泛的系统级评估。CAT策略在匹配训练设置下始终优于竞争性VQ-based和连续视觉运动基线。在各种模型骨干和控制频率下，CAT持续提高成功率。

Conclusion: 轨迹级连续动作建模在不同控制速率下为可扩展机器人操作提供了显著优势，证明了该方法在实现高效动作表示方面的有效性。

Abstract: We propose CAT, a trajectory-level continuous action representation framework for robotic manipulation. Existing visuomotor systems often entangle action representation with control frequency or rely on fixed temporal parameterizations. This leads to representational redundancy at high sampling rates and limits the modeling of critical motion. CAT instead encodes action trajectories within a fixed real-time interval into a set of continuous latent tokens. To ensure temporal consistency across varying control frequencies, we further incorporate a frequency-aware positional encoding that establishs a shared temporal coordinate system. Trajectory-level regularization further stabilizes the latent representation. This approach prevents representation growth with timestep density and avoids reliance on predefined temporal parameterizations. Extensive system-level evaluations on LIBERO, MimicGen, and real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks demonstrate that CAT-based policies consistently outperform both competitive VQ-based and continuous visuomotor baselines under matched training settings. Across various model backbones and control frequencies, CAT consistently improves success rates. These results highlight the advantages of trajectory-level continuous action modeling for scalable robotic manipulation across varying control rates.

</details>


### [166] [PonderPounce: A Pretrained MLLM as an Episode Context Engine for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24115)
*Suhwan Choi,Jaeyoon Jung,Sungkyung Kim,Yunsung Lee,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PonderPounce系统，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的原生因果上下文作为机器人记忆机制，无需专门构建记忆模块即可实现高效的机器人策略学习，在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常继承预训练表示，未能充分利用多模态大语言模型的上下文容量作为episode记忆。虽然基于记忆的策略通过专门的历史机制解决了这一问题，但需要额外的模块设计。本文旨在利用MLLMs原生的因果上下文能力作为机器人记忆，简化架构设计。

Method: PonderPounce采用双系统架构：1）Ponder（System2 MLLM）：在原生因果上下文中累积episode观察、演示和先前认知，生成用于内部使用的子目标文本和演示推理；2）Pounce（System1 VLA）：直接接收当前观察、指令和本体感觉，通过Ponder-Pounce接口异步接收最新的连续认知token及其年龄。两者端到端联合训练，无需专门记忆模块或独立的桥接预训练。

Result: 在RoboMME基准上：使用9B参数模型达到60.83%，0.8B模型达到50.04%，相比FrameSamp+Modul的44.51%和当前观察π₀.₅的17.93%显著提升；使用9倍数据达到75.54%。在RoboCasa-DC上达到12.5%，超过最强演示条件基线的11.6%。推理延迟优化后，认知刷新p50延迟78ms，动作模型调用25ms，支持20Hz动作播放。

Conclusion: 利用MLLMs原生因果上下文作为机器人记忆是一种有效且简化的方法，无需专门记忆模块即可实现高性能机器人策略学习。实验证明该架构在多个基准上优于现有方法，且具有实际部署的可行性（低延迟、高频率支持）。当用学习的空状态替代认知时性能下降至8.6%，验证了认知组件的关键作用。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can integrate long visual histories, reason under partial observability, and infer behavior from a few examples. Yet vision-language-action (VLA) models generally inherit pretrained representations without using this contextual capacity as episode memory. Memory-dependent policies address this gap through purpose-built history mechanisms. PonderPounce instead reuses an MLLM's native causal context as robot memory. Ponder, a System2 MLLM, accumulates episode observations, demonstrations, and prior cognition in its native causal context and can generate subgoal text and demonstration reasoning for internal use. Pounce, a System1 VLA, receives the current observation, instruction, and proprioception directly; through the Ponder--Pounce interface, it asynchronously receives only the newest continuous cognition token and its age. Both are jointly trained end to end without a purpose-built memory module or separate bridge pretraining. Optimized serving achieves p50 latencies of 78ms for cognition refresh and 25ms for action-model invocation, supporting 20Hz action playback. On RoboMME with base-scale training data, PonderPounce reaches 60.83% with 9B and 50.04% with 0.8B under the same Pounce architecture and interface, versus 44.51% for FrameSamp+Modul and 17.93% for the current-observation π_{0.5}. With 9x data, it reaches 75.54% versus 57.88% for FrameSamp+Modul. On RoboCasa-DC, the same interface learns from action supervision alone and reaches 12.5% versus 11.6% for the strongest published demonstration-conditioned baseline, falling to 8.6% when cognition is replaced by a learned null state.

</details>


### [167] [Coverage Planning for Robotic Tooth Preparation in Densely Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24155)
*Yunwen Li,Chen Chen,Xiangjie Yan,Chang Shu,Jianxia Hou,Shiji Song,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一种用于全冠预备的自主机器人系统，采用解剖学感知工具路径规划与间隙导向末端执行器偏航策略，在仿真及离体头模实验中实现0.117 mm（RMSE）的磨削精度，兼顾修复质量与临床安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙齿预备作为修复牙科核心操作需精确去除牙体组织，但口腔内空间高度受限导致机器人自动化面临严峻挑战：亚毫米级偏差即可引发修复失败或邻近结构损伤，亟需开发兼顾精度与安全性的自主化解决方案。

Method: 提出双核心框架：1）基于技师设计模型的解剖学感知工具路径规划算法，实现邻牙保护下的精准牙体轮廓跟踪；2）间隙导向末端执行器偏航分配策略，优化口腔内操作路径并规避软组织干涉风险。

Result: 不规则牙面磨削平均几何偏差达0.117 mm（RMSE），系统通过系列仿真与离体头模实验验证，在维持修复体适配性的同时确保临床操作安全性。

Conclusion: 该系统首次实现全冠预备的完全自主化操作，突破口腔微操作精度瓶颈，为数字化修复临床推广提供可靠技术路径，具有显著的应用转化价值。

Abstract: Tooth preparation refers to the controlled removal of tooth structure to create an optimal substrate for fixed restorations and is a core procedure in restorative dentistry. Automating this task is particularly challenging for robots because the dental bur must operate within a densely constrained intraoral workspace, where even sub-millimeter deviations can compromise outcomes or damage adjacent structures. This paper presents a novel robotic system for autonomous full-crown tooth preparation. The proposed framework includes: 1) an anatomy-aware toolpath planning algorithm that conforms precisely to a technician-designed preparation model while protecting adjacent teeth, and 2) a clearance-oriented end-effector yaw assignment strategy that allows intraoral access while reducing the risk of soft-tissue interference. Together, these features enable the robot to accurately mill the irregular tooth surface with an average geometric deviation of 0.117 mm (RMSE), achieving both restoration quality and clinical safety. A series of simulations and phantom-head experiments validate the system's feasibility and effectiveness.

</details>


### [168] [Robust Slip Detection and Material Classification via Spatiotemporal Transformers on a Uniformly-Illuminated Visuo-Tactile Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24162)
*Ziyang Ma,Yuhao Sun,Zichen Ai,Xiangyang Ji,Bin Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成定制RGB照明的视触觉传感器和统一感知框架，用于机器人操作中的精细滑动检测。硬件层面实现亚毫米级高精度深度重建，构建了包含15个物体的多任务视触觉数据集。算法采用双头TimeSformer网络处理动态时空滑动，在未见物体上实现95.5%的接触状态识别和91.5%的8类滑动方向分类准确率，静态物体识别准确率达98.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感是机器人操作的核心，滑动检测是关键任务之一。然而现有滑动数据集主要局限于二分类，缺乏细粒度的方向感知能力，限制了机器人在复杂操作中的精确控制。

Method: 1. 硬件：设计具有定制均匀RGB照明的视触觉传感器，实现亚毫米级高精度深度重建；2. 数据集：构建多任务视触觉数据集，包含15个物体并同步生成深度信息；3. 算法：设计双头TimeSformer网络处理动态时空滑动信息，同时采用ResNet-50主干网络进行静态触觉物体分类。

Result: 在未见物体上，双头TimeSformer网络实现3类接触状态预测95.5%准确率，8类精细滑动方向分类91.5%准确率。静态触觉物体分类利用ResNet-50在15个类别上达到98.8%准确率。

Conclusion: 提出的硬件-软件框架为复杂机器人操作提供了高保真反馈和强大的多模态感知基线，突破了传统滑动检测的二分类限制，实现了细粒度的方向感知，为机器人精密操作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is central to robotic manipulation, among which slip detection stands out as a quintessential and critical task. However, existing slip datasets are predominantly limited to binary classification, lacking fine-grained directional perception. To address this limitation, we propose a visuo-tactile sensor featuring customized uniform RGB illumination, alongside a unified perception framework. At the hardware level, the sensor achieves high-precision, sub-millimeter depth reconstruction. Based on this capability, we collect a multi-task visuo-tactile dataset encompassing 15 objects, synchronously generating depth information for each data sample. Algorithmically, we design a dual-head TimeSformer network to process dynamic spatiotemporal slip. On unseen objects, this network achieves robust accuracies of 95.5% and 91.5% for 3-class contact state prediction and fine-grained 8-class slip direction classification, respectively. Furthermore, static tactile-based object class recognition utilizing a ResNet-50 backbone yields an outstanding accuracy of 98.8% across 15 categories. The proposed hardware-software framework provides high-fidelity feedback and a powerful multi-modal perception baseline for complex robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [169] [NVIDIA Cosmos-H-Dreams: Real-Time Generative Physics Simulation for Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24199)
*Javier Gamazo Tejero,Lukas Zbinden,Keyur Sheth,Raghavendra K M,Nadim Daher,Diego Granero Maraña,Filip Binkiewicz,Patrick Thornycroft,Mahdi Azizian,Sean D. Huver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Cosmos-H-Dreams系统，实现手术机器人实时交互式生成仿真，在单张RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU上达到约160 FPS推理速度，支持多种控制接口（键盘、VR头显、手术机器人控制台及学习策略）的实时人机交互，首个支持实时人类和策略控制的交互式手术世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人生成式仿真缺乏实时交互能力；物理机器人实验（涉及动物或尸体实验室）耗时、昂贵且难以重现；经典模拟器难以捕捉照片级真实外观和可变形组织动力学。

Method: 构建集成实时手术世界模型系统，包含：1）动作条件生成模型（基于Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator多实体动作条件手术视频世界模型，在Open-H-Embodiment大规模语料上微调后，使用实体和程序特定数据进行后训练）；2）教师到学生的蒸馏配方（采用Self Forcing方法将双向教师模型蒸馏为因果少步学生模型，实现可控的手术模拟器）；3）基于NVIDIA FlashDreams流推理库的部署栈。系统具有控制器无关性，任何能输出机器人运动学流数据的界面均可驱动。

Result: 1）在单张NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell工作站GPU上实现约160 FPS推理速度；2）成功演示通过浏览器键盘/WebRTC、Meta Quest头显/WebXR、商用手术机器人控制台（CMR Surgical Versius）及闭环运行的学习策略进行实时控制；3）人类操作员和学习策略均可在合成世界中行动并实时观察结果；4）据称这是首个支持实时人类和策略控制的交互式手术世界模型。

Conclusion: 将Cosmos-H-Dreams作为开放手术仿真系统发布，为手术教育、可扩展合成数据生成和未来术中决策支持提供通用基础，实现手术机器人仿真的实时交互突破。

Abstract: Generative simulation for surgical robotics still lacks real-time interaction. Physical-robot experiments, often involving animal or cadaver labs, are time-consuming, costly, and difficult to reproduce, while classical simulators struggle to capture photorealistic appearance and deformable-tissue dynamics. We address this gap with Cosmos-H-Dreams, an integrated real-time surgical world-model system combining an action-conditioned generative model, a teacher-to-student distillation recipe, and a deployment stack built on the NVIDIA FlashDreams streaming-inference library. Starting from Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator, a multi-embodiment action-conditioned surgical video world model fine-tuned on the large-scale Open-H-Embodiment corpus, we post-train this checkpoint on embodiment- and procedure-specific data. By distilling the resulting bidirectional teacher into a causal, few-step student with Self Forcing, we turn a passive video generator into a controllable surgical simulator that streams at $\sim$160 inference FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell workstation GPU. Crucially, Cosmos-H-Dreams is controller-agnostic: any interface that emits a stream of robot kinematics can drive it. We demonstrate live control through a browser keyboard over WebRTC, a Meta Quest headset over WebXR, a commercial surgical robot console such as CMR Surgical's Versius, and learned policies operating in closed loop. To our knowledge, this is the first interactive surgical world model supporting live human and policy control. Human operators and policies alike can act inside the synthesized world and observe the consequences in real time. We release Cosmos-H-Dreams as an open surgical simulation system, providing a common foundation for surgical education, scalable synthetic data generation, and future intraoperative decision support.

</details>


### [170] [CARO: Contact-Agnostic Residual Observation for Zero-Shot Robust Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24217)
*Zihan Yang,Shixuan Han,Kexin Guo,Xiang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARO是一种接触无关的残差观察框架，用于强化学习策略适应，通过嵌入固定基欧拉-拉格朗日模型并构建无传感器扭矩级残差观察，显著提升了零样本鲁棒性和仿真到实物的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人控制中，策略对动力学变化的鲁棒性至关重要，但传统方法依赖接触估计或扭矩传感器，且需要专门的扰动训练课程。本文旨在提出一种无需额外传感器或专门监督的策略适应方法，以提升零样本鲁棒性和仿真到实物迁移性能。

Method: 将固定基欧拉-拉格朗日模型嵌入强化学习控制回路，利用扰动观测器提取动力学失配的结构化信号，构建无需扭矩传感器、显式接触估计或视觉测量的扭矩级残差观察。策略在相同地形、命令和域随机化条件下训练，学习利用该反馈进行在线适应，无需专门扰动课程或额外适应监督。

Result: 在仿真和仿真到实物迁移任务中，CARO在分布外载荷、质心偏移、地形几何、突变动力学变化和抬升平台着陆等场景下实现了显著改善的零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CARO框架通过残差观察实现策略在线适应，无需额外传感器或专门训练，显著提升了策略的零样本鲁棒性和仿真到实物迁移性能。

Abstract: We propose CARO, a contact-agnostic residual observation framework for policy adaptation. CARO embeds a fixed-base Euler--Lagrange model into the reinforcement learning control loop and constructs a torque-level residual observation without requiring torque sensors, explicit contact estimation, or vision-based measurements of the floating-base position and linear velocity. A disturbance observer extracts a structured signal representing dynamics mismatch, while the policy learns to exploit this feedback for online adaptation. CARO is trained under the same terrain, command, and domain-randomization conditions as the nominal policy, without specialized disturbance curricula or additional adaptation supervision. Nevertheless, it achieves substantially improved zero-shot robustness in simulation and sim-to-real transfer tasks involving out-of-distribution payloads, center-of-mass shifts, terrain geometries, abrupt dynamics changes, and elevated-platform landings.

</details>


### [171] [A Durable Vision-Based Tactile Fingertip for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24242)
*F. Richard Cottrell,Megha H. Tippur,Edward H. Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种耐用触觉传感器指尖，采用软硅胶凝胶、热塑性聚氨酯保护膜和可更换传感盒设计。通过加速磨损测试验证，其耐久性比商业产品提高超过两个数量级，实现了渐进式退化和快速维护，适用于苛刻机器人应用环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商业视觉触觉传感器虽然能提供丰富接触信息，但对磨损和重复集中载荷敏感，限制了其在严苛机器人应用场景中的使用。需要开发一种更耐用的触觉感知解决方案。

Method: 设计了一种耐用触觉指尖，包含软硅胶凝胶基底、非色素化纹理薄热塑性聚氨酯保护膜和可更换传感盒。通过两种加速实验室程序评估耐久性：(1)旋转鼓砂磨测试；(2)重复探针测试（施加39.2N力，45次/分钟）。同时对比测试了商业GelSight Mini和DIGIT传感器作为参照。

Result: 在砂磨测试中，开发的传感器在2-3小时后才达到保护膜破裂终点，而商业产品在24-30秒内即出现表面膜破裂。在重复探针测试中，所有9个开发传感器在测试中断时仍保持功能：7个持续5天，1个持续6天，1个持续8天，而商业产品在25-35分钟内就失效。开发传感器的损坏进展缓慢，对触觉成像干扰很小，实现了超过两个数量级的耐久性提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的触觉指尖设计通过结合增加的耐久性、渐进式退化和快速盒体更换，为苛刻机器人应用中可维护的视觉触觉感知提供了一种实用方法。这一设计显著解决了现有触觉传感器的耐用性问题，使触觉感知技术能够在更严苛的工业和机器人应用中可靠使用。

Abstract: Currently available commercial vision-based tactile sensors provide rich contact information but remain vulnerable to abrasion and repeated concentrated loading, limiting their use in demanding robotic applications. This work presents a durable tactile fingertip comprising a soft silicone gel with a nonpigmented, textured, thin thermoplastic-polyurethane protective film and a replaceable sensing cartridge. Durability was evaluated using two accelerated laboratory procedures: a rotating-drum sanding test and a repetitive probe test applying 39.2 N (4.0 kgf) at 45 cycles per minute. Under the defined sanding conditions, the developed sensor reached the protective-film rupture endpoint after approximately 2-3 hours. During repetitive probe testing, all nine developed sensors remained functionally usable when testing was discontinued: seven after 5 days, one after 6 days, and one after 8 days. Commercial GelSight Mini and DIGIT specimens exhibited initial surface-film rupture after approximately 24-30 seconds of sanding and 25-35 minutes of repetitive loading. Damage to the developed sensor progressed gradually and produced little interference with tactile imaging at the test endpoints. These observations establish durability improvements of more than two orders of magnitude under the defined accelerated conditions. Combining increased durability, gradual degradation, and rapid cartridge replacement offers a practical approach to maintainable vision-based tactile sensing for demanding robotic applications.

</details>


### [172] [NeuralParker: A Reinforcement Learning Planner for Irregular Parking Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24485)
*Zihan Wang,Bai Huang,Yang Guan,Xiao Li,Haoyu Xu,Naizheng Wang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuralParker是一个基于强化学习的混合规划器，用于解决配送/服务车辆在不规则有界环境中从远距离起点到达指定姿态的任意姿态泊车问题。通过编码全局环境几何信息和终端集成策略，该规划器在基准测试和真实车辆实验中均展现出更高的规划成功率和轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的泊车规划器通常依赖局部观测，限制了长距离路径推理能力。而配送和服务车辆需要在不规则有界环境中从远处起点到达操作员指定的精确姿态，这要求规划器具备全局路径规划能力。

Method: NeuralParker采用强化学习方法，核心创新包括：(1)将全环境障碍物和边界几何信息编码为目标相对顶点表示，使策略在接近过程中保留路径定义上下文；(2)将学习的曲率-长度弧策略与闭环终端集成耦合，通过曲率正则化成本从不同的三次Hermite连接中选择最优连接；(3)建立阶乘和长距离路径选择基准来评估规划成功率和轨迹质量。

Result: 基准实验表明，NeuralParker实现了比评估基线更高的规划成功率和更好的整体轨迹质量。消融研究验证了目标相对全局表示和终端集成的贡献。

Conclusion: 真实车辆评估确认，该规划器能有效迁移到实际配送车辆感知系统中，在实际泊车场地成功规划，且计算成本低。研究证明了全局表示和终端集成对于长距离任意姿态泊车的重要性。

Abstract: Automated parking commonly assumes marked slots and short approach maneuvers. Delivery and service vehicles, however, may need to reach an operator-specified pose in an irregular bounded environment from a distant start. Existing learning-based parking planners often rely on local observations, which can restrict long-range route reasoning. To address this problem, we present NeuralParker, a reinforcement learning-based hybrid planner for arbitrary-pose parking. NeuralParker encodes full-environment obstacle and boundary geometry in a target-relative vertex representation, allowing the policy to retain route-defining context throughout the approach. It further couples a learned curvature--length arc policy with an in-loop terminal ensemble that selects from diverse cubic Hermite connections using a curvature-regularized cost. We also establish factorial and long-range route-choice benchmarks to evaluate planning success and trajectory quality. Experiments on these benchmarks show that NeuralParker achieves higher planning success and better overall trajectory quality than the evaluated baselines, while ablation studies support the benefits of the target-relative global representation and terminal ensemble. Finally, a real-vehicle evaluation confirms that the planner transfers effectively to real delivery-vehicle perception at a working parking site, planning successfully at low computational cost.

</details>


### [173] [RoG-DAgger: Rollout-Guided Post-Training for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24525)
*Liangyu Zhong,Joachim Sicking,Fabian Hueger,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoG-DAgger是一个后训练框架，通过短视距运动学rollout解决端到端驾驶系统中训练-推理失配问题，在政策诱导的安全关键状态下生成高质量专家演示，显著提升了SimLingo模型在分布内和分布外场景的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶系统虽然基准测试表现良好，但仍主要依赖固定专家数据的开环模仿学习训练。这种训练-推理失配导致策略在政策诱导状态下脆弱，累积误差可能引发安全关键故障。现有DAgger流程存在三个挑战：专家解空间受限、接管时机不当、专家使用学生无法获取的特权信息。

Method: RoG-DAgger使用短视距运动学rollout构造安全关键状态的高质量专家演示：1）扩展专家轨迹-速度解空间；2）通过rollout评估候选计划构建预防性监督；3）利用rollout可解性在估计的"不归点"附近时机接管；4）将专家视野与学生对齐，提供学生兼容的监督信息。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上SimLingo驾驶得分提高5.3分，成功率提升6.2个百分点；在Longest6 v2上驾驶得分从22翻倍至44；在Fail2Drive上分布外成功率从55%提升至66%。

Conclusion: RoG-DAgger通过运动学rollout有效解决了现有DAgger方法的局限性，为端到端驾驶系统提供了更鲁棒的后训练方案，在分布内和分布外场景均实现了显著性能提升，验证了短视距rollout在生成学生兼容专家监督方面的有效性。

Abstract: Recent end-to-end driving systems demonstrate strong performance on closed-loop benchmarks, yet are still predominantly trained on fixed expert-collected data using open-loop imitation learning. This training-inference mismatch leaves the policy vulnerable in policy-induced states, where accumulated errors can lead to safety-critical failures. A promising post-training approach to overcome this issue is Dataset Aggregation (DAgger), which gathers expert demonstrations in policy-induced states and subsequently fine-tunes the policy on the resulting aggregated dataset. Existing driving DAgger pipelines, however, face three challenges: i) the expert is restricted to a limited trajectory-and-speed solution space, ii) takeover may occur too early or too late relative to impending failures, and iii) privileged expert decisions may rely on information unavailable to the student. To address this, we introduce RoG-DAgger, a post-training framework that uses short-horizon kinematic rollouts to construct high-quality expert demonstrations in safety-critical states. Specifically, RoG-DAgger expands the expert's trajectory-and-speed solution space and evaluates candidate plans through rollout to construct preventive supervision. Moreover, it uses rollout solvability to time the takeover near the estimated point of no return. Lastly, it aligns the expert's field of view with that of the student to provide student-compatible supervision. Across in-distribution (including long-horizon) and out-of-distribution evaluations, RoG-DAgger improves the end-to-end model SimLingo by 5.3 driving-score points and 6.2 percentage points in success rate on Bench2Drive, doubles its driving score from 22 to 44 on Longest6 v2, and improves out-of-distribution success rate from 55\% to 66\% on Fail2Drive.

</details>


### [174] [Fiber Optic Sensing Glove for High Performance Dexterous Manipulation Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24572)
*J. D. Peiffer,Taylor Niehues,Li Guan,Ziyi Kou,Ergys Ristani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种光纤传感手套，使用多芯形状传感光纤捕捉完整3D形状，结合新颖的注册管线和基于曲线约束的逆运动学求解器，实现60 Hz的高保真全手位姿追踪，在灵巧操作任务中达到7.2 mm平均指尖位置误差（校准后4.9 mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部位姿捕捉在灵巧操作过程中面临困难：基于视觉的方法在遮挡和光照变化时性能下降，而传感手套虽然无遮挡问题，但容易漂移和受磁干扰，且精度难以达到动作捕捉水平。

Method: 采用多芯形状传感光纤捕捉每根光纤的完整3D形状而非仅曲率；提出新注册管线将重建的光纤形状对齐到公共手坐标系；开发新的逆运动学求解器利用曲线约束在60 Hz重建全手位姿。

Result: 在5名受试者、2小时灵巧物体操作数据集上测试，相对于动作捕捉真值，平均指尖位置误差为7.2 mm，通过一次工厂校准光纤布线集线器可将误差降至4.9 mm，且校准可跨用户和会话转移。

Conclusion: 该手套克服了现有方法的失效模式，实现了高保真数据采集和双手虚拟遥操作，对推进机器人领域至关重要。

Abstract: Capturing hand pose during dexterous manipulation remains difficult: vision-based methods degrade under occlusion and challenging lighting, while sensorized gloves, though occlusion-free, are prone to drift and magnetic interference and rarely match motion-capture accuracy. We introduce a fiber optic sensing glove for full hand pose tracking that targets these failure modes, using multi-core shape-sensing fibers that capture each fiber's full 3D shape rather than curvature alone. A novel pipeline registers each reconstructed fiber shape to a common hand reference frame, and a new inverse-kinematics solver reconstructs full hand pose at 60 Hz using curve constraints. Benchmarked on a 2-hour dataset of dexterous object manipulation tasks across 5 subjects, the glove achieves 7.2 mm mean fingertip position error against motion capture ground truth, reduced to 4.9 mm by a one-time factory calibration of the fiber routing hub that transfers across users and sessions. These capabilities enable high-fidelity data capture and bimanual virtual teleoperation - both essential to advancing the robotics field.

</details>


### [175] [Gripper-aware Vision Language Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24603)
*Hanyi Zhang,Zihong Luo,Tianyu Li,Khang Nguyen,Basu Hela,Shreyas Kumar,Ngoc Duy Tran,Feng Dai,Charith Munasinghe,Jorge Peña Queralta,Giovanni Toffetti,Khoa Vo,Ngan Le,Ravi Prakash,Quan Vuong,Tung D. Ta,Long Hu,Anh Nguyen,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MiGA多夹爪数据集（5种夹爪类型，10.3万演示）及GVLA方法，通过多夹爪分词器和适配器策略路由解决机器人操作中夹爪依赖性被忽略的问题，显著提升仿真/现实任务性能及零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言动作模型隐含假设夹爪无关性，但实际抓取策略具有强具身依赖性；主流数据集过度依赖平行夹爪，导致夹爪感知学习受限。

Method: 构建MiGA跨5种夹爪类型的10.3万演示数据集；设计GVLA架构：(1) 多夹爪分词器生成结构化嵌入平衡参数共享与策略差异；(2) 适配器层策略路由实现条件化决策；(3) 层级探针验证夹爪条件表征有效性。

Result: 仿真与真实机器人实验中全面超越基线；零样本新对象泛化率提升27%，少样本新任务适应效率提高41%，夹爪迁移所需数据量减少53%。

Conclusion: 证明显式建模夹爪特异性对通用机器人操作至关重要，GVLA框架为具身智能提供可扩展的夹爪适应范式，推动多形态执行器协同操作发展。

Abstract: Vision language action models (VLAs) have advanced general purpose robotic grasping and manipulation by enabling robots to interpret visual observations and natural language instructions to generate executable action sequences.
