However, existing VLAs often implicitly assume gripper invariance, despite grasping strategies being inherently embodiment-dependent. Different gripper types, such as parallel-jaw and suction, usually require distinct interaction strategies to achieve the same grasping objective. Moreover, current datasets for VLAs predominantly rely on parallel-jaw grippers, limiting gripper-aware learning. To address this gap, we introduce MiGA, a multi-gripper-aware dataset spanning five distinct gripper types across multiple robots with 103,000 demonstrations, explicitly capturing strategy divergence under shared task objectives. We further propose GVLA, which combines a new multi-gripper tokenizer with adapter-based policy routing. Our new gripper encoding induces structured embedding information that balances parameter sharing and strategy differentiation, while layer-wise probing confirms meaningful gripper-conditioned representations for VLAs. Intensive experiments in both simulation and real-world robots show that our GVLA outperforms the current baselines across evaluated settings. Our method also improves zero-shot generalization or few-shot adaptation to new objects or unseen tasks, and enable more efficient gripper adaptation.

</details>


### [176] [VIP: Variation-based Iterative-learning Planning for Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24618)
*Shuli Lv,Pengda Mao,Chen Min,Li Hong,Runxiao Liu,Shuai Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新型变分迭代学习规划(VIP)框架，通过无限维函数空间直接更新连续规划命令，避免传统方法中高维离散化与预测时域扩展导致的计算瓶颈。在保持单次迭代O(n)复杂度的前提下，实现单机器人及集群的高效运动规划，并通过仿真与实测验证其有效性、效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在复杂障碍物密集环境中的运动规划面临严峻挑战：传统基于有限维轨迹参数化或长预测时域的方法计算成本随问题规模急剧增长，尤其在高密度多机器人场景下难以满足实时性要求，且受限于机载计算资源。亟需突破现有计算瓶颈以支持规模化部署。

Method: 核心创新在于提出变分迭代学习规划(VIP)框架：① 将规划问题建模为无限维函数空间中的连续函数优化，摒弃离散轨迹变量优化；② 设计变分更新机制，支持离线模型环(microcontroller-in-the-loop)与在线物理执行环(robot-in-the-loop)双模式；③ 通过空间离散点数量n控制计算复杂度，实现单步O(n)复杂度。该方法适用于不同规划目标、机器人平台及集群配置。

Result: 大规模仿真与真实世界实验表明：VIP在多样化场景(单机器人/集群、不同运动学平台、多种规划目标)中均能高效生成并迭代优化运动规划。关键指标包括：计算时间显著低于传统方法(避免时域扩展开销)，规划成功率提升，集群规模扩展时仍保持实时性。具体数据体现其作为通用规划方法的有效性、计算效率与可扩展性。

Conclusion: VIP框架通过革命性地转变规划范式(从离散变量优化转向连续函数变分更新)，根本性解决了传统运动规划的计算复杂度问题。其实证表现确认了该方法的普适适用性，为高密度障碍物环境下单机器人及机器人集群的高效安全导航提供了可推广的解决方案，具有重要工程应用价值。

Abstract: Over the past decade, autonomous robotic systems have been increasingly deployed in applications such as surveying, search and rescue, and last-mile delivery. These applications require robots to generate safe and efficient motion plans in large, complex, and obstacle-dense environments, often under limited onboard computing resources. However, conventional planning methods commonly rely on finite-dimensional trajectory parameterization or increasingly long prediction horizons, leading to rapidly growing computational costs, particularly in multi-robot scenarios. This paper presents a novel variation-based iterative-learning planning (VIP) framework for efficient motion planning of both single robots and robotic swarms. Instead of optimizing a large number of discrete trajectory variables, VIP directly updates the planning command as a continuous function in an infinite-dimensional function space. The same variation-based update can be implemented in a model-in-the-loop manner for offline planning or in a robot-in-the-loop manner between online physical executions. By avoiding the computational burden associated with horizon expansion and high-dimensional trajectory discretization, VIP maintains a per-iteration computational complexity of $\mathcal{O}(n)$, where $n$ denotes the number of spatial discretization points. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can efficiently generate and iteratively improve motion plans for different planning objectives, robotic platforms, and swarm configurations, highlighting its effectiveness, computational efficiency, and scalability as a general planning methodology.

</details>


### [177] [GaussianWAM: Distilling Geometry and Semantics from 3D Gaussian Fields into World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24714)
*Zijian Zhang,Yuqing Jiang,Weitao Zhou,Minglei Li,Jinhao Zhang,Yao Mu,Xiaofan Li,Hao Zhao,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GaussianWAM，一种训练时表示增强框架，通过3D高斯场统一组织几何与语义监督信号，蒸馏进WAM的当前观测表示，显著提升机器人操作任务性能，且训练后移除所有辅助模块，保持原始WAM部署架构不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）的视频隐变量主要优化视觉预测，未显式鼓励保留跨视图几何结构或空间局部化的物体相关语义，导致表示质量受限，影响下游操作性能。

Method: 利用冻结的几何与视觉基础模型，从同步多视图观测中提取深度、相机参数和密集语义特征；将这些异质信号绑定至共享高斯原语，渲染空间对齐的语义、深度及覆盖目标；通过蒸馏将目标注入WAM的当前观测表示。训练完成后移除所有教师模型、高斯组件与辅助预测头。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上，FastWAM从52.05%提升至71.29%，Cosmos Policy从71.52%提升至77.30%；直接CLIP与VGGT蒸馏的FastWAM基线为69.37%，经高斯场统一后进一步提升至71.29%；在标准LIBERO、RoboTwin及真实世界操作上均显示正向迁移。

Conclusion: 训练时高斯蒸馏为向WAM表示注入几何与语义相关监督提供了一种实用途径，无需修改部署架构即可显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) jointly learn future visual prediction and action generation, using video dynamics as a representation-learning signal for robotic manipulation. However, their video latents are primarily optimized for visual prediction and are not explicitly encouraged to preserve cross-view geometric structure or spatially localized, object-relevant semantics. We propose \textbf{GaussianWAM}, a training-time representation-enhancement framework that organizes geometric and semantic supervision through a 3D Gaussian field. Given synchronized multi-view observations, frozen geometry and vision foundation models provide depth, camera parameters, and dense semantic features. GaussianWAM binds these heterogeneous signals to shared Gaussian primitives and renders spatially aligned semantic, depth, and coverage targets, which are distilled into the current-observation representations of the WAM. All teacher models, Gaussian components, and auxiliary prediction heads are removed after training, leaving the original WAM inference path without additional modules or forward computation. On LIBERO-Plus, GaussianWAM improves FastWAM from 52.05\% to 71.29\% and Cosmos Policy from 71.52\% to 77.30\%. Direct CLIP and VGGT distillation already establishes a strong FastWAM baseline of 69.37\%, while Gaussian-field unification further improves it to 71.29\%, supporting the benefit of spatially organizing heterogeneous teacher signals. GaussianWAM also improves performance on standard LIBERO and shows positive transfer trends on RoboTwin and real-world manipulation. These results suggest that training-time Gaussian distillation provides a practical way to inject geometry- and semantics-related supervision into WAM representations without changing their deployment architecture.

</details>


### [178] [Fiber Bragg Grating Whiskers for Bioinspired Hydrodynamic Perception on Underwater Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24724)
*Hao Li,Tianyu Tu,Siyue Yao,Ziyang Chang,Juhyun Jung,Xiaochi Xie,Long Yin Chung,Tian-Ao Ren,Genliang Chen,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受海豹胡须启发，研究团队开发了光纤光栅须状传感器，使水下机器人能够感知流体轨迹。实验验证该传感器可有效检测涡流并指导机器人导航决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海豹利用须状毛感知水中移动的物体，即使在黑暗或浑浊水域也能被动感知目标。研究旨在将这种生物感知能力移植到水下机器人中，提高其环境感知能力。

Method: 设计紧凑型光纤光栅（FBG）须状传感器，模拟海豹胡须的非均匀锥度和椭圆形横截面特征。通过拖曳实验、俯仰翼实验和机器人导航实验系统评估传感器性能。

Result: 传感器在0.1-0.6 m/s流速下表现出单调相对流动响应；与圆柱基线相比显著减少自激振荡；对攻角有明显依赖性；能检测运动源产生的特征涡流，在源通过后数秒仍可检测；单个前装传感器使机器人在20次试验中17次（85.0%）正确选择分支。

Conclusion: 生物启发流体感知技术与机器人行动成功对接，证明了须状传感器在水下物体跟踪中的实用性，为水下机器人自主导航提供了新的感知方案。

Abstract: Harbor seals track hydrodynamic trails with their vibrissae, enabling passive perception of moving targets in dark or turbid water. Inspired by this capability, we present compact fiber Bragg grating (FBG) whiskers for underwater robots. Like seal whiskers, they have a non-uniform taper and elliptical cross-section. Controlled towing experiments show a monotonic relative-flow response from 0.1 to 0.6 m/s, a strong reduction of self-induced oscillation relative to a cylindrical baseline, and a pronounced dependence on angle of attack. Experiments with a pitching foil show that the whiskers can detect the characteristic vortices shed by a stationary or moving source, detectable several seconds after the source has passed. Using this information, a single front-mounted whisker enabled a small underwater robot to distinguish between continuing straight and executing a turn, selecting the correct branch in 17 of 20 trials (85.0%) from whisker signals alone. These results connect bioinspired hydrodynamic sensing to robot action and suggest the utility of whiskers for tracking underwater objects.

</details>


### [179] [One-Shot Learning from Demonstration of Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation by Identifying Physical Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24741)
*A. H. G. Overbeek,H. van der Kooij,M. Vlutters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种显式建模物理交互（接触/脱离）的示教学习方法，通过混合位置-力控制器实现接触丰富的操作任务再现，在单次示教下实现复杂任务的鲁棒执行、泛化与在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有示教学习(LfD)方法大多未显式建模机器人与环境间的物理交互（如接触形成与脱离），而这些交互在操作任务中至关重要；同一类基础物理交互频繁重现，可作为鲁棒、泛化与自适应任务执行的基础。

Method: 提出一种显式利用物理交互发生位置与时机的LfD方法：使用混合位置-力控制器跟踪示教轨迹，直到满足示教中的接触基础转换条件；在真实机器人实验中评估，包括开门锁、拧螺丝、撬起、表面轮廓检测等任务。

Result: 实验表明显式建模物理交互带来四方面优势：(1)仅需单次示教且无需先验任务知识即可复现复杂、顺序性、接触密集的操作任务；(2)对环境几何未知变化具有鲁棒性；(3)在已知几何变化下实现泛化；(4)利用任务执行中探索的几何信息实现在线适应。

Conclusion: 通过显式实现鲁棒性、泛化性与自适应性（通常LfD文献中缺失），本文致力于填补可解释的小样本机器人操作LfD的空白，为接触密集型任务的少次示教学习提供新范式。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) allows robots to learn manipulation tasks directly from humans, thereby supporting the versatile application of robots. Most LfD methods do not explicitly model the physical interactions between a robot and its environment, such as the making and breaking of contact, while these are crucial during manipulation tasks. Because the same basic physical interactions recur often, they can be a basis for robust, generalizable, and adaptive task reproduction. We propose an LfD method that explicitly uses what physical interactions take place where and when. Using that information, a hybrid position-force controller tracks demonstrated trajectories until contact-based transition conditions from the demonstrations are met. We evaluate our method in real robot experiments consisting of opening doors and locks, bolt picking and screwing, dislodging, and surface contouring. We show that explicitly modeling physical interactions benefits LfD in four ways. First, by allowing reproduction of complex, sequential, and contact-rich manipulation tasks using only a single demonstration and no prior knowledge of the task. Second, by facilitating robustness to unknown geometric variations in the environment. Third, by facilitating generalization when geometric variations are known. Fourth, by facilitating online adaptation using geometric information explored during task reproduction. We discuss how robustness, generalization, and adaptivity can be explicitly implemented, which is generally lacking in the LfD literature. Thereby, our work aims to close a gap in interpretable few-shot LfD of robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [180] [Latent Action as Intention Enables Efficient Future Imagination for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24882)
*Xiang Li,Yupeng Zheng,Songen Gu,Huailiang Ma,Feng Yu,Xian Nie,Shanshuai Yuan,Yujie Zang,Weize Li,Shuai Tian,Moyang Liu,Ya-Qin Zhang,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAWA 是一种新的世界动作模型 (WAM) 架构，通过紧凑的潜行动表示替代未来观测生成，在保持高效推理的同时实现了更好的泛化性能，在 RoboCasa 等基准上达到最先进成功率，并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 通过在测试时生成未来观测来改进机器人控制，但导致高延迟；Fast-WAM 虽移除该过程以提升效率，却泛化能力不足，尤其在机器人演示稀缺和分布外场景。需要一种既能高效推理又能保留未来想象能力的方案。

Method: LAWA 使用离散 tokenizer（通过无行动预训练增强）生成操作中心的码本目标，联合去噪一个锚定于这些目标的连续潜状态与可执行动作块，在推理时省略未来视频分支，从而以紧凑潜行动表示未来意图，实现测试时高效未来想象。

Result: 在 RoboCasa 上，LAWA 在 few-shot 和全数据设置下分别达到 65.6% 和 80.8% 的最先进平均成功率，相比匹配 Fast-WAM 基线提升 9.6 和 4.5 个点；同时保持 Joint-WAM 变体的性能水平，推理延迟降低 42.9%；在 LIBERO-Plus 上展示竞争性零样本鲁棒性，在真实世界任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: 未来想象不必被丢弃：通过紧凑潜行动保留它，能在性能、泛化和延迟之间实现有效权衡。代码和模型将发布。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) improve robot control by modeling how observations evolve, but generating future observations at test time incurs substantial latency. Fast-WAM removes this process for efficiency; however, our matched implementations show lower generalization for Fast-WAM than for future-aware alternatives, especially with scarce robot demonstrations and in out-of-distribution scenarios. To bridge this gap, we introduce **LAWA**, a WAM architecture that uses compact latent actions as an operational representation of future intentions, enabling efficient test-time future imagination without generating future observations. Specifically, a discrete tokenizer enhanced by action-free pre-training produces manipulation-centric codebook targets. LAWA jointly denoises a continuous latent state anchored to these targets with executable action chunks while omitting the future-video branch at inference. On RoboCasa, LAWA achieves state-of-the-art average success rates of 65.6% and 80.8% in the few-shot and full data settings, improving over the matched Fast-WAM baseline by 9.6 and 4.5 points, respectively. It also preserves the performance level of the matched Joint-WAM variant while requiring 42.9% lower inference latency. LAWA also demonstrates competitive zero-shot robustness on LIBERO-Plus and superior performance on real-world tasks. These results show that future imagination need not be discarded: retaining it with compact latent actions yields an effective trade-off among performance, generalization, and latency. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [181] [Do Robotic World Models Really Follow Actions? Diagnosing and Aligning Action-Conditioned Generation for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24885)
*Sixiang Chen,Jiaming Liu,Jixian Wu,Yichen Guo,Tinghao Wang,Siyuan Qian,Hao Chen,Jiajun Cao,Jian Tang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldEcho基准和WorldSync方法，用于诊断和改进世界模型在非专家动作下的动作遵循能力，结果表明WorldSync能显著提升世界模型的可靠性，进而提高策略迭代的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前动作条件化的世界模型作为策略评估和改进的学习模拟器日益重要，但其有效性依赖于一个未经验证的假设：生成的未来状态能真实反映任意有效动作。现有基准通常局限于专家演示数据，导致对非专家动作的遵循能力缺乏充分评估，这一局限性阻碍了世界模型在实际应用中的可靠性和泛化性。

Method: 1. WorldEcho：一个诊断性基准，通过在更广泛的动作分布上探测动作遵循能力，使用视觉完整性和SE(3)轨迹对齐度进行量化评估。

2. WorldSync：从三个互补维度增强动作遵循能力的方法：
   - 分布覆盖：扩展训练动作后果的分布范围
   - 表征接地：通过动作强制专家（Action-Forcing Expert）将中间视频表征与动作引起的机器人动力学关联
   - 干预效果对齐：使动作干预下的预测变化与真实未来状态的相应变化保持一致

Result: 诊断发现现有世界模型能合理执行专家动作，但在处理多样化非专家轨迹时表现不佳，要么忽略指定动作，要么产生视觉上无效的模拟结果。在RoboTwin基准和真实机器人任务上的实验表明，WorldSync改善了WorldEcho指标，能作为更可靠的模拟器用于迭代策略改进，使策略达到更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 世界模型的非专家动作遵循能力是制约其作为可靠模拟器的关键瓶颈。通过WorldEcho基准识别问题，WorldSync方法在分布覆盖、表征接地和干预效果对齐三个维度上的改进，显著提升了世界模型的动作遵循能力，证明了增强这些能力对于实现可靠的策略评估和改进至关重要，为机器人学习中的世界模型应用奠定了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are increasingly used as learned simulators for policy evaluation and improvement, yet their effectiveness rests on an unverified assumption: generated futures faithfully reflect arbitrary valid actions. Existing benchmarks are typically confined to expert demonstrations, leaving off-expert action following inadequately evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce WorldEcho, which probes action following over a broader action distribution using visual integrity and SE(3) trajectory alignment. Our diagnosis shows that current world models reasonably execute expert actions but struggle with diverse off-expert trajectories, either ignoring the commanded actions or producing visually invalid rollouts. We further propose WorldSync, which strengthens action following along three complementary axes: distributional coverage, representational grounding, and intervention-effect alignment. It broadens the training distribution over action consequences, grounds intermediate video representations in action-induced robot dynamics through an Action-Forcing Expert, and aligns predicted changes under action interventions with the corresponding changes in ground-truth futures. Experiments on RoboTwin benchmarks and real-robot tasks show that WorldSync improves WorldEcho metrics and serves as a more reliable simulator for iterative policy improvement, enabling policies to achieve higher success rates.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Receive Diversity for Differential Binary Noise Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23950)
*Paulo V. B. Tomé,André A. dos Anjos,Hugerles S. Silva,Daniel C. Araújo,Robson D. Vieira,Ertugrul Basar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出差分子噪声(DBN)调制的SIMO接收方案，通过决策统计量组合实现精确误比特率分析，无需高斯近似。研究表明分集阶数受限于min(Mμ,N)，并给出了最优权重的闭式解，65 GHz实测验证了空间资源与观测长度的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分子噪声调制需要避免信道状态信息和载波相位恢复，以提高系统鲁棒性。多天线接收可进一步提升性能，但需要优化组合策略以平衡复杂度和性能。

Method: 采用后相关决策统计量组合的单入多出接收方案，推导复高斯向量的厄米二次型统计量，获得精确的条件误比特率表达式。对κ-μ衰落和复用能量进行平均，得到任意权重下的精确BEP。求解偏转最优权重闭式解。

Result: 1)获得无需高斯近似的精确条件BEP；2)分集阶数受观测长度限制为min(Mμ,N)；3)偏转最优权重在软组合和功率加权之间插值；4)65 GHz室内NLoS测量结果量化了空间资源与观测长度的权衡。

Conclusion: 差分子噪声调制的SIMO接收通过决策统计量组合实现了精确性能分析，最优权重方案有效平衡了系统复杂度和性能，测量结果验证了理论分析的正确性，并为系统参数优化提供了依据。

Abstract: Differential binary noise (DBN) modulation encodes information in the polarity transition of a reused noise realization, dispensing channel state information and carrier-phase recovery. This letter proposes single-input multiple-output (SIMO) reception for DBN via post-correlation decision-statistic combining, followed by one zero-threshold decision. The combined statistic is a Hermitian quadratic form in complex Gaussian vectors, yielding an exact conditional bit error probability (BEP) with no Gaussian approximation. Averaging it over $κ$-$μ$ fading and the reused energy gives the exact BEP for arbitrary weights. Because that energy is shared, its deep fades are common to every branch, and the observation length caps the diversity order at $\min(Mμ,N)$. The deflection-optimal weights follow in closed form and interpolate soft combining and power weighting. Measured 65 GHz indoor non-line-of-sight (NLoS) results quantify the trade-off between spatial resources and observation length.

</details>


### [183] [Physics-Informed WiFi Sensing for Robust 3D Human Pose Estimation in Mobile and Cross-Environment Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23995)
*Kaixuan Huang,Yuanbo Chen,Guangjin Pan,Shiyi Mu,Tao Yu,Guhan Zheng,Shunqing Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息驱动的WiFi感知框架，用于在移动和跨环境场景下实现鲁棒的3D人体姿态估计。该方法通过显式建模无线信号传播特性、引入多径感知注意力机制以及解耦表征学习，显著提升了系统在异构环境中的鲁棒性和泛化能力，优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于商用WiFi信号的非设备式人体姿态估计在移动计算系统中具有广阔前景，但现有方法在部署到异构环境时，由于复杂的多径传播和无线信号域偏移，性能会严重下降。因此，需要一种能够适应不同环境变化、具有强泛化能力的鲁棒姿态估计方法。

Method: 提出了一个物理信息驱动的WiFi感知框架。该方法显式建模无线信号传播特性，并引入多径感知注意力机制来捕捉与环境相关的信号变化。此外，引入了解耦表征学习方案，将姿态相关特征与环境特定因素分离，从而实现无需大量重新训练的跨域自适应。

Result: 使用商用WiFi设备实现了该系统，并在多个公开基准（Person-in-WiFi-3D和MM-Fi）以及真实世界多样化室内环境中进行了评估。实验结果表明，该框架在鲁棒性和泛化性能上显著优于最先进方法，尤其是在跨环境场景下。

Conclusion: 物理信息驱动的无线感知为在移动计算系统中实现可靠、可扩展、无基础设施的人体中心应用展示了巨大潜力。

Abstract: Device-free human pose estimation using commodity WiFi signals has emerged as a promising paradigm for pervasive sensing in mobile computing systems. However, existing approaches often suffer from severe performance degradation when deployed across heterogeneous environments due to complex multipath propagation and domain shifts in wireless signals. In this paper, we present a physics-informed WiFi sensing framework for robust 3D human pose estimation under mobile and cross-environment settings. Our approach explicitly models wireless signal propagation characteristics and incorporates multipath-aware attention to capture environment-dependent signal variations. To further improve generalization, we introduce a disentangled representation learning scheme that separates pose-related features from environment-specific factors, enabling effective cross-domain adaptation without requiring extensive retraining. We implement our system using commodity WiFi devices and evaluate it on multiple public benchmarks, including Person-in-WiFi-3D and MM-Fi, as well as real-world deployments across diverse indoor environments. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly improves robustness and generalization performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, particularly under cross-environment scenarios. These results highlight the potential of physics-informed wireless sensing for enabling reliable, scalable, and infrastructure-free human-centric applications in mobile computing systems.

</details>


### [184] [Impact of Optical Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces on Channel Estimation for VLC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24287)
*Borja Genoves Guzman,M. Julia Fernández-Getino García*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: ORIS在非理想信道估计下显著提升VLC性能，PSAM方法实现约5 bits/s/Hz频谱效率，优于理想CSI无ORIS场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光通信受链路阻塞限制，现有ORIS研究依赖理想信道状态信息假设，缺乏实际非理想场景验证。

Method: 采用PSAM和ST两种基于导频的信道估计技术，分析数据功率分配因子及平均OFDM符号对VLC系统性能的影响，评估ORIS部署效果。

Result: PSAM结合ORIS实现高数据功率分配，频谱效率超越理想CSI无ORIS情况；ST在平均OFDM符号多时表现良好但不及PSAM；系统对ORIS方向不完美具鲁棒性；可达5 bits/s/Hz频谱效率。

Conclusion: ORIS与PSAM结合在非理想条件下实现高效VLC通信，性能媲美无阻塞视距链路，验证其实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Visible light communication (VLC) offers wireless data connectivity indoors, yet its performance is severely limited by link blockages. While optical reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (ORISs) have been proposed to overcome these blockages, existing literature predominantly relies on the unrealistic assumption of ideal channel state information. In this paper we investigate the contribution of ORIS elements in VLC systems under practical, non-ideal channel estimation scenarios. We analyze the communication performance using two pilot-based estimation techniques: pilot symbol assisted modulation (PSAM) and superimposed training (ST), evaluating the impact of data power allocation factors and averaged orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) symbols. Our results show that PSAM with an ORIS deployment enables optimal large data power allocation factors, resulting in spectral efficiency improvements that surpass scenarios with ideal channel estimates but without ORIS deployment. While ST performs well with a large number of averaged OFDM symbols, it is unable to overcome the performance of PSAM when ORIS elements are deployed. We also find the system robust to imperfect ORIS orientation; even with only half of the elements accurately aligned, performance remains acceptable. Ultimately, we show that spectral efficiency values of approximately 5 bits/s/Hz are achievable using ORIS with PSAM, a performance comparable to, or even exceeding, that of unobstructed line-of-sight links.

</details>


### [185] [Sensor Drift Compensation via Olfactory system and Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24288)
*ZhengChen Dong,ChenWei Li,Takeaki Yajima*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）和脉冲随机池计算（SRC）的样本级在线漂移补偿方法，利用STDP实现无标签特征适应，结合WTA竞争机制进行自监督分类，在真实数据集上显著提升了对抗传感器漂移的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子鼻在医疗诊断与工业控制中应用前景广阔，但传感器漂移严重削弱了其长期可靠性。现有补偿方法多基于批处理，缺乏非平稳环境下的连续在线学习能力；现有在线方法多依赖准在线小批量学习，缺乏对无缓冲的真实样本级在线学习的探索。

Method: 提出基于SNN和SRC的样本级在线漂移补偿框架。特征适应阶段利用脉冲时间依赖可塑性（STDP）机制，实现无标签的时空吸引子动力学自组织（STDP-FA）；分类阶段采用SRC，通过胜者通吃（WTA）竞争驱动自监督适应，无需缓冲区即可逐样本处理。

Result: 该方法能够有效处理渐变漂移、随机漂移、传感器故障及突变等多种概念漂移模式。在真实世界传感器漂移数据集上的仿真结果表明，其分类精度显著优于现有的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了无缓冲的样本级在线漂移补偿，突破了传统批处理与准在线方法的局限，有效提升了电子鼻在非平稳环境下的长期感知可靠性与适应性，为复杂动态环境下的智能传感提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Despite the promising applications of electronic noses (e-Noses) in medical diagnosis and industrial process control, sensor drift remains a critical challenge that degrades long-term sensing reliability by inducing gradual shifts in sensor responses. Conventional drift compensation methods are typically designed for batch learning and lack the ability to support continuous online learning in non-stationary environments. Although several online drift compensation methods have recently been proposed, they are mainly based on quasi-online mini-batch learning for distribution adaptation, while true sample-wise online learning without buffering remains largely unexplored. To address these issues, this paper proposes a sample-wise online drift compensation method based on spiking neural networks (SNNs) for feature adaptation and spiking reservoir computing (SRC) for classification. By exploiting spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), the SNN self-organizes spatiotemporal attractor dynamics for label-free feature adaptation (STDP-FA), and the adapted features are classified by SRC with self-supervised adaptation driven by winner-take-all (WTA) competition. The proposed method addresses various concept drift patterns, including gradual drift, random drift, sensor failures, and abrupt changes. Simulations on a real-world sensor drift dataset demonstrate a clear improvement in classification accuracy over baseline methods.

</details>


### [186] [Predicting Only from Selected Evidence: A Tempered Product-of-Experts Bottleneck for Auditable EEG Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24377)
*Yinghao Wang,Shujian Yu,Duc Han Le,Zhikai Yu,Changming Wang,Van-Tam Nguyen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出tPoE-EIB，一种证据信息瓶颈头，通过选择时间与通道证据、映射到高斯专家并用调温专家乘积后验融合，使分类器仅依赖受限潜变量进行预测，从而实现可审计的EEG诊断；在六类任务上保持竞争性准确率的同时，显著提升选择与集成忠实度审计表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练EEG骨干网络虽提升了迁移性能，但下游诊断头仍难以审计：预测基于无约束隐藏状态，解释通常在决策后才产生。需要一种能实现显式、可审计决策路径的诊断头，以弥补这一缺口。

Method: tPoE-EIB首先选择时间与通道证据，将选择后的摘要映射到共享潜变量上的高斯专家，再用调温专家乘积后验融合这些专家。分类器仅观察融合后的潜变量，决策路径因此显式且受后验KL信息速率限制。该方法形成可解析的监督目标，并加入信息速率惩罚；调温后验缓解因相关证据轴导致的过度自信融合。

Result: 在六类诊断任务（事件类型分类、异常检测、癫痫检测、认知衰退分期、抑郁筛查、脑血管病分类）上评估，涵盖公开基准与内部临床队列、二分类筛查与细粒度分期、稀疏与密集导联。tPoE-EIB保持竞争性平衡准确率，并在选择忠实度审计（插入-删除、门因果测试）上优于代表性事后解释；其结构化后验还支持集成忠实度审计（专家删除、后验依赖、专家分歧测试）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于证据唯一、速率受限的融合是实现在冻结EEG基础骨干上可审计诊断的实用途径，兼顾性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Pretrained EEG backbones improve transfer performance, but downstream diagnosis heads remain hard to audit: predictions are made from unrestricted hidden states, whereas explanations are usually produced only after the decision. We introduce tPoE-EIB, an evidence-information bottleneck head for adapting EEG backbones under an evidence-only prediction constraint. tPoE-EIB selects temporal and channel evidence, maps the selected summaries to Gaussian experts over a shared latent variable, and fuses them with a tempered product-of-experts posterior. The classifier observes only this latent, so the decision path is explicit and rate-limited by the expected posterior KL. This gives a tractable supervised objective with an information-rate penalty, while the closed-form tempered posterior mitigates overconfident fusion from correlated evidence axes. We evaluate tPoE-EIB on pretrained EEG foundation-model backbones across six diagnosis settings: event-type classification, abnormality detection, seizure detection, cognitive-decline staging, depression screening, and cerebrovascular-disease classification. The evaluation spans public benchmarks and in-house clinical cohorts, binary screening and fine-grained staging, and sparse and dense montages. tPoE-EIB preserves competitive balanced accuracy and improves over representative post-hoc explanations on selection-faithfulness audits, including insertion-deletion and gate-causality tests. Its structured posterior further enables integration-faithfulness audits, including expert-drop, posterior-reliance, and expert-disagreement tests. Overall, these results suggest that evidence-only, rate-limited fusion is a practical route to auditable diagnosis on top of frozen EEG foundation backbones.

</details>


### [187] [EM-KalmanNet: Learned Expectation-Maximization for Adaptive Tracking in Partially Known, Block-Wise Time-Varying State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24404)
*Ori Cohen,Nir Shlezinger,Tirza Routtenberg*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: State estimation in partially known state space (SS) models is challenging when the dynamics or observation model varies across short data blocks. Classical model-based approaches, such as the expectation-maximization (EM) Kalman filter, jointly recover the latent states and the unknown model parameters, but rely on linear-Gaussian assumptions that should accurately describe the system and require numerous forward-backward passes. Consequently, their performance and computational efficiency may deteriorate under complex and non-stationary real-world conditions. On the other hand, learned Kalman smoothers are robust to model mismatch yet cannot adapt at inference to unseen model variations without labeled data. In this work, we propose EM-KalmanNet, an AI-aided tracking algorithm for adaptive smoothing in blockwise time-varying SS models. The method unfolds a fixed, small number of EM-like iterations into a trainable architecture: a parameter-aware RTSNet implements a learned E-step conditioned on the current model-parameter estimate, while a lightweight M-Net implements a learned M-step that updates the state-transition or the observation matrix using empirical moments, residuals, and gradient-related statistics. The two modules are shared across the unfolded iterations and are trained offline via a dedicated three-stage procedure. During deployment, the per-block parameter estimate is propagated between consecutive blocks, enabling observation-driven adaptation without labeled online data or knowledge of the noise statistics. Experiments involving linear and nonlinear models, Gaussian and non-Gaussian noise, Lorenz attractor tracking, and acoustic source localization demonstrate that EM-KalmanNet consistently outperforms model-based and data-driven benchmarks while substantially reducing inference latency relative to the EM-KF.

</details>


### [188] [Time-Window Noise2Noise: A Self-Supervised Method for Blind Denoising of Vibration and Impact Signals in Mechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24443)
*Vinicius S. Vianna,Tiago H. Machado,Ilmar F. Santos*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督的盲去噪方法Time-Window Noise2Noise (TWN2N)，用于机械系统信号去噪，无需先验噪声信息或干净参考信号，即可在极低信噪比条件下显著恢复模态参数等关键量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械系统的振动与冲击测量不可避免地受到噪声污染，导致模态参数、接触力等衍生量退化。经典滤波器需要预先知道噪声统计特性，且在极低信噪比或彩色噪声场景下往往失效。

Method: TWN2N采用自监督学习框架，通过掩码-重建机制：对每个时间点的邻域进行掩码并强制自编码器重建被掩码点，使其学习系统动态而非噪声。无需干净参考信号、配对噪声观测或噪声先验表征。方法适用于任何确定性动力系统，推理时单次前向传递，计算轻量。

Result: 在两个已知真实值的基准上评估：线性三自由度系统脉冲响应和非线性Hunt-Crossley冲击。跨白噪声、粉红噪声、棕噪声及量化噪声，在重复实现中SNR提升具有统计显著性（p<0.001）。与最优调谐的Savitzky-Golay和VisuShrink小波滤波器对比，在15 dB输入信噪比下，去噪后原本不可识别的模态参数恢复到参考值的4%误差范围内。

Conclusion: TWN2N为确定性机械系统的盲去噪提供了一种通用、高效的方法，无需噪声先验知识即可显著改善信号质量，并将益处传导至参数识别环节，使在原始噪声信号中无法辨识的模态参数得以恢复。

Abstract: Vibration and impact measurements in mechanical systems are invariably corrupted by noise, which degrades every quantity derived from them, such as modal parameters and contact forces. Classical filters attenuate noise only when its statistics are known a priori and often fail at very low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) or under spectrally coloured noise. This work presents Time-Window Noise2Noise (TWN2N), a self-supervised method for blind denoising of signals produced by deterministic mechanical systems. The method requires neither a clean reference signal nor paired noisy observations, nor any prior characterization of the noise: by reconstructing each time instant from its temporal neighbourhood while masking the instant itself, a compact autoencoder is forced to learn the underlying dynamics rather than the noise. The formulation is general for any deterministic dynamical system, is computationally lightweight (a single feed-forward pass per sample at inference), and is assessed on two benchmarks with exactly known ground truth: the impulse response of a linear 3-degree-of-freedom system and a nonlinear Hunt-Crossley impact. Across white, pink, brown and quantization noise, and over repeated realizations, TWN2N yields statistically significant SNR gains (p < 0.001) without any prior noise information, and it is benchmarked against optimally-tuned Savitzky-Golay and wavelet (VisuShrink) filters. The benefit propagates to identification: at 15 dB input SNR, modal parameters that are unidentifiable from the raw signal are recovered within 4% of their reference values after denoising.

</details>


### [189] [A Transformer for Joint Multi-Receiver Pilotless Wi-Fi Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24584)
*Xavier Tardy,Grégoire Lefebvre,Apostolos Kountouris,Haïfa Farès,Amor Nafkha*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自注意力Transformer的全无导频多接入点Wi-Fi接收机，在三个接入点的配置下，实现了约10⁻⁵的码字误码率，同时通过消除导频提高了频谱效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导频信号占用宝贵的频谱资源，降低了系统的频谱效率。开发无导频通信机制可以在不牺牲可靠性的前提下提高频谱利用率，这对于现代高密度无线环境具有重要意义。

Method: 设计了一种基于自注意力Transformer的无导频接收机架构，该架构对每个（接入点，子载波）对生成令牌，输出用于标准低密度奇偶校验（LDPC）译码器的逐比特logits。系统利用多接入点提供的空间分集实现可靠通信。

Result: 在真实射线追踪室内信道中，单一接入点配置无法达到实用误码率；而采用三个接入点的协同配置，能够达到约10⁻⁵的码字误码率，与具有完美信道知识的理想最大比合并基线相当。

Conclusion: 在存在空间分集的条件下，无导频解码成为可行方案。虽然单接入点配置性能不足，但多接入点协同配置能够实现实用级的误码率性能，同时通过消除导频显著提升频谱效率，为下一代高效无线通信提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper we discuss the development of a fully pilotless multi-access-point Wi-Fi receiver. This is based on a self-attention Transformer that operates on per-(access point, subcarrier) tokens and outputs bit-wise logits for a standard low-density parity-check decoder. In realistic ray-traced indoor channels, pilotless decoding becomes feasible as soon as some spatial diversity is available. While a single-access point configuration does not attain practical error rates, when compared to an ideal maximum-ratio-combining baseline with perfect channel knowledge, cooperative configurations with three access points achieve coded bit error rates of approximately $10^{-5}$. This is achieved while benefiting from higher spectral efficiency by eliminating pilots.

</details>


### [190] [An Ultra-Compact Differential V-Band Power Amplifier Using EDMOS Transistors With 18.1 dBm P1dB and 21% PAE in 22nm FD-SOI CMOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24686)
*Han Zhou,Torgil Kjellberg,Haojie Chang,Christian Fager*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper presents a compact, fully differential, two-stage millimeter-wave (mm-wave) cascode power amplifier (PA) designed and implemented in a 22nm FD-SOI CMOS process (22FDX+). The PA employs the newly introduced extended-drain MOS (EDMOS) device in 22FDX+, together with a carefully engineered device core and transformer baluns. At 50 GHz, the prototype achieves 18.8 dBm saturated output power (PSAT), 18.1 dBm 1-dB compression output power P1dB, and 21% power-added efficiency (PAE) at P1dB. To the best of our knowledge, this work achieves the highest reported power density of 2.6 W/mm2 among single-way, two-stage CMOS cascode PAs.

</details>


### [191] [An Efficient W-/D-Band Power Amplifier in a 130 nm SiGe BiCMOS Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24687)
*Han Zhou,Yu Yan,Haojie Chang,Herbert Zirath*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Infineon Technologies 130nm SiGe BiCMOS工艺的宽带功率放大器，工作在W波段上部至D波段下部频段。采用负载牵引仿真和带通滤波器匹配网络设计方法，实现了71-133GHz的增益带宽，增益平坦度优于1dB，饱和输出功率8.8-11.7dBm，峰值功率附加效率7.2-11.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证SiGe BiCMOS技术在W/D波段宽带和集成发射前端应用中的潜力，为毫米波频段通信系统提供高性能功率放大器解决方案。

Method: 1. 采用Infineon Technologies 130nm SiGe BiCMOS工艺实现
2. 执行完整的负载牵引仿真方法
3. 采用基于带通滤波器(BPF)的匹配策略设计输出和级间匹配网络

Result: 1. 小信号增益3dB带宽：71-133GHz
2. 在75-128GHz范围内增益相对平坦，约15.7dB，波动小于1dB
3. 测量饱和输出功率：8.8-11.7dBm
4. 测量峰值功率附加效率(PAE)：7.2-11.1%

Conclusion: 所设计的功率放大器展示了SiGe BiCMOS技术在W-/D波段频率范围内的宽带和集成发射前端的巨大应用潜力，为毫米波通信系统提供了高性能、高集成度的功率放大解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a wideband power amplifier (PA) designed and implemented in Infineon Technologies' 130-nm SiGe BiCMOS process for upper W-band and lower D-band applications. A complete load-pull simulation methodology is carried out, and a band pass filter (BPF)-based matching strategy is employed for the design of the output and inter-stage matching networks. The fabricated PA prototype achieves a small-signal gain 3-dB bandwidth of 71-133 GHz. Moreover, it maintains a relatively flat gain of approximately 15.7 dB over 75-128 GHz, with less than 1-dB fluctuation. The measured saturated output power is 8.8-11.7 dBm, while the measured peak power-added efficiency (PAE) is 7.2-11.1%. These results demonstrate the potential of SiGe BiCMOS technology for wideband and integrated transmitter front ends operating across the W-/D-band frequency range.

</details>


### [192] [Relativistic Cramér-Rao Bound Scaling for Device-Based and Device-Free Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24722)
*Fan Liu,Yifeng Xiong,Weijie Yuan,Yuanhao Cui,Jie Yang,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究相对论运动下基于设备(DB)和无设备(DF)传感的测距与测速问题，建立统一仿射信号模型，推导解析Cramér-Rao下界，揭示超相对论速度下测距和测速精度的渐近行为及两种模式的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低速近似在相对论速度下失效，需要精确描述单向/双向传播引起的时缩放与多普勒效应，以评估测距和测速的根本精度极限，并为高速场景下的传感系统设计提供理论指导。

Method: 通过推导相对论运动下时缩放与时移的精确关系，将DB和DF两种模式统一为仿射信号模型；进而基于Fisher信息矩阵推导出Cramér-Rao下界的闭式表达式，显式表示为归一化速度、均方根带宽和均方根时长的函数。

Result: 所得CRB在低速时回归经典结果；在超相对论区域呈现独特速度标度：快速远离时测距CRB发散而测速CRB趋零；快速靠近时两者均趋零。DB与DF模式在两种运动方向上表现出不同的渐近阶数。

Conclusion: 相对论运动不仅改变了信号模型结构，也根本性地改变了决定测距与测速精度的标度律，表明高速传感必须重新审视其理论框架。

Abstract: This letter investigates range and velocity estimation under relativistic motion for device-based (DB) and device-free (DF) sensing. By deriving the exact time-scaling and time-shift relations induced by one-way and two-way propagation, both sensing modes are cast into a unified affine signal model. Closed-form Cramér--Rao bounds (CRBs) are obtained as explicit functions of normalized velocity, root-mean-squared (RMS) bandwidth, and RMS duration. The bounds recover the classical low-speed results but exhibit distinct velocity scaling in the ultrarelativistic regime. For rapidly receding motion, the range CRB diverges while the velocity CRB vanishes. For rapidly approaching motion, both CRBs vanish. The DB and DF modes further exhibit different asymptotic orders in the two directions, showing that relativistic motion changes not only the signal model but also the fundamental scaling laws governing sensing accuracy.

</details>


### [193] [Rotatable Antenna Relaying: Joint Precoding and Antenna Pointing Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24798)
*Ying Gao,Qingqing Wu,Wen Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究可旋转天线增强型半双工放大转发中继系统，通过联合优化基站/中继预编码矩阵及旋转天线指向矩阵，最大化多用户系统中最小SINR性能，提出基于流形感知Frank-Wolfe和交替优化的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升半双工中继系统频谱效率并消除跨跳重定向延迟与控制开销，研究利用共享指向矩阵的定向可旋转天线协同多天线基站和单天线用户间的通信性能。

Method: ① 单用户场景下利用闭式最优中继预编码降维为旋转天线指向优化；② 采用流形感知Frank-Wolfe算法求解指向矩阵；③ 多用户场景通过二次变换重构等效问题，结合对数求和指数平滑的MFW扩展算法在交替优化框架中更新指向矩阵。

Result: 仿真验证所提算法在SNR/SINR方面均优于对比方案，揭示：最优旋转指向适应双跳几何结构；最佳方向性因子随用户负载变化；均衡双跳传播条件时中继位置更优。

Conclusion: 共享指向矩阵的旋转天线机制可显著提升中继系统性能，所提优化方法有效平衡各用户服务质量，研究成果为智能天线中继网络设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates a rotatable antenna (RA)-enhanced half-duplex amplify-and-forward relaying system, where a multi-antenna base station (BS) serves multiple single-antenna users via a multi-antenna relay. The BS and users employ isotropic antennas, whereas the relay employs directional RAs whose pointing matrix is shared by both hops to avoid inter-hop reorientation delay and control overhead. We aim to maximize the minimum signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) among all users by jointly optimizing the BS precoding, relay precoding, and RA pointing matrices. To tackle this non-convex problem, we first investigate the single-user scenario and reduce the joint design to RA pointing optimization using the optimal relay precoding matrix available in closed form. A manifold-aware Frank--Wolfe (MFW) method is then employed to obtain a suboptimal pointing solution. Under a symmetric far-field line-of-sight geometry, we further characterize the globally optimal pointing structure and derive a directivity threshold separating common pointing from antenna splitting. Building on this MFW procedure, we next address the general multiuser scenario. Specifically, the quadratic transform is first applied to obtain an equivalent auxiliary-variable formulation, which is subsequently solved suboptimally via alternating optimization (AO). In particular, we employ a safeguarded extension of the MFW method based on log-sum-exp smoothing to update the RA pointing matrix in each AO iteration. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed algorithms consistently achieve the best signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and SINR performance among all considered schemes. It is further shown that the optimized RA pointing adapts to the two-hop geometry, the preferred directivity factor depends on user load, and relay placement with relatively balanced two-hop propagation conditions is generally preferable.

</details>


### [194] [Coherent Direct D-MIMO Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24880)
*Benjamin J. B. Deutschmann,Lukas D'Angelo,Erik Leitinger,Klaus Witrisal*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的高斯过程滤波器族，用于分布式MIMO系统中的位置估计，通过贝叶斯状态空间滤波和粒子信念传播方法，实现了非相干、相干和载波相位处理，并证明了相干处理能显著提升定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式MIMO系统具有定位潜力，但严格频率同步和相位校准要求以及多模态似然函数阻碍了估计过程，现有算法大多非相干处理牺牲了定位精度。

Method: 提出了基于集中式Type-I和边际Type-II似然的贝叶斯状态空间滤波器族，推导了后验Cramér-Rao下界(PCRLB)，开发了粒子信念传播方法实现并行计算。

Result: 实验显示所提滤波器接近各自PCRLB，相干处理显著优于非相干处理；GPU加速实现每步运行时间为数十毫秒，计算复杂度线性扩展。

Conclusion: 提出了统一的定位框架，揭示了相干性与相位参数的关系，验证了方法有效性，为未来无线系统定位提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed multiple-input multiple-output (D-MIMO) is envisioned as a key deployment architecture for future wireless systems, offering improved coverage and robustness through spatial separation, and favorable geometry for localization and sensing. Its greatest potential for localization lies in joint coherent processing across distributed antenna panels. However, stringent frequency-synchronization and phase-calibration requirements, together with multimodal likelihood functions, hinder the estimation process. Consequently, most existing algorithms process the panels noncoherently, potentially sacrificing localization accuracy. We present a unified family of Bayesian state-space filters that are based on concentrated Type-I and marginal Type-II likelihoods for wideband near-field D-MIMO systems and operate directly on noisy channel observations. The Type-I filters explicitly realize (i) noncoherent, (ii) coherent, and (iii) carrier-phase-based processing. For Type-II filtering, we show that a zero-mean model is inherently noncoherent under distributed processing, whereas observation stacking restores coherence. A nonzero-mean model can automatically adapt to the coherence available in the data, a property that we term ``soft coherence''. We derive posterior Cramér-Rao lower bounds (PCRLBs) for all three coherence levels and show that each level is fundamentally tied to the number of phase parameters used for positioning or treated as nuisance parameters. Numerical results show that the coherence-specific filters closely approach their respective PCRLBs and that coherent processing can substantially outperform noncoherent processing. We derive particle-based belief propagation methods, which parallelize over particles and distributed panels, scale linearly with the observed data, and achieve runtimes of tens of milliseconds per time step in a GPU-accelerated implementation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入RENDER基准控制，在固定对话内容的同时系统性地改变读者端呈现格式，揭示记忆/RAG任务中不同输入格式对模型性能的显著影响，并建议评估中应报告或控制输入格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆和RAG评估将模型输入格式视为实现细节，忽视了不同系统可能以不同方式呈现相同历史（如记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录），这种差异可能显著影响模型性能，但评估中未予控制或报告。

Method: 提出RENDER基准控制，固定对话内容同时系统性改变读者端呈现格式。采用五级数据包阶梯定位答案内容进入输入的时间点，结合确定性模板近似四种常见格式：ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT风格类型化记录和原始对话。

Result: 在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上实验：匹配预算的解析数据包比近期截断的原始对话高出42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板中每模型最佳最差差距为24.6-48.8分；主评分器下ChatGPT风格条目在9个模型中的7个上优于原始对话；三个在正式账本数据包上得分为0%的模型从自然语言条目中回答相同事实达到45.4-53.4%；效应在检索噪声下持续存在并迁移到HotpotQA。

Conclusion: 读者端呈现格式显著影响记忆/RAG任务中的模型性能。评估应报告或控制输入格式，忽略此因素可能导致性能比较误导。RENDER基准为格式影响的系统评估提供了框架。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [196] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ESQ-Bench，一个基于Oracle的企业级NL2SQL基准测试，揭示了当前AI模型与企业数据库需求之间的显著差距，Claude Sonnet 4.6在所有复杂度层级上均优于GPT-4o。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NL2SQL基准测试（如Spider和BIRD）依赖简化的学术模式和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的复杂性，需要构建更接近真实企业场景的基准测试来评估模型的实用性。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench基准测试，包含：6个填充模式（465张表，164,682行），跨Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL和SQL Server四个数据库系统；三层复杂度分级，共550个金标准验证的问答对；四指标评估框架（EM、EX、SR、SD）；采用系统化的复杂度分级和静默分歧评估方法。

Result: 1. GPT-4o使用模式链接提示时执行准确率呈单调下降：79.8%、60.3%、57.2%（各层级）；2. EM全层级低于7%；3. EX通过查询的静默分歧率达73-99%；4. Claude Sonnet 4.6表现更优：87.4%、74.9%、68.7%；5. GPT-4o零样本在2-3层级因执行率较低和幸存者偏差导致性能反转；6. Llama 3.2仅达13.3%全库EX准确率；7. 高层级错误主要由错误结果语义主导。

Conclusion: 当前NL2SQL模型与企业数据库需求存在显著差距，闭源API模型显著优于开源权重模型，即使在最高表现模型中也难以应对复杂企业模式，表明企业级SQL生成仍面临重大技术挑战。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [197] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多智能体框架，使LLM智能体能够利用制药工艺仿真模型进行受控实验，通过干预、比较和观察进行推理，从而生成比纯语言推理更具体、可操作性更强且准确率更高的工艺参数优化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽在推理、规划等方面表现出色，但许多科学和工程任务需要超越文本生成的能力，特别是理解系统在干预下的响应，这在实际中依赖于受控实验。

Method: 构建多智能体框架，将语言模型与高保真仿真模型集成在交互式智能体架构中：给定用户查询和基线配置后，系统构建结构化任务表示，设计实验，执行对比仿真，解释结果，并综合基于证据的工艺参数优化建议。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该系统在输出具体性、用户评价的正确性和有用性方面均优于纯语言推理方法，消融实验和可视化案例分析进一步验证了仿真集成实验推理的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 将LLM与科学仿真模型结合的受控实验推理框架能够显著提升模型在制药工艺设计等复杂科学任务中的推理能力，产生更具体、可操作且准确的输出，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [198] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 审计发现AION-1模型严重依赖调查分割图而非图像像素，继承了目录不完整性偏差。剔除检测通道可消除偏差且无成本，但tokenizer存在分辨率和量化限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文基础模型训练于调查像素和衍生目录，目录的不完整性会被模型继承为系统偏差。需审计AION-1以量化其对目录的依赖及偏差影响。

Method: 采用因果干预法：固定图像token仅编辑分割图以观察输出变化；分析检测门控机制（中心位置vs光强）；模拟管道缺失率（3.68%）对红移和位置误差的波及；测试光谱数据和剔除检测通道的影响；评估tokenizer状态分辨率及量化限制；检验稀疏字典作为因果句柄的可靠性。

Result: 编辑分割图使报告量变化110-4400倍；模型依赖中心位置存在性(r=0.47)而非光强(r=0.30)，混合源分析显示忽略光强分割(R=-0.006)；矛盾目录使性能比无元数据差9倍；管道缺失导致红移中位数偏差达LSST DESC要求的0.71倍，位置误差最坏达8.3倍；光谱数据或剔除检测通道可消除偏差且无成本，偏差随模型规模增大；图像编码器仅28种有效状态(光谱934种)，红移受量化限制；稀疏字典恢复率不稳定(26-75%)。

Conclusion: AION-1过度依赖目录分割图导致系统偏差，影响红移和位置精度并超出LSST要求。剔除检测通道或使用光谱可消除偏差且无损性能。模型还受限于tokenizer分辨率及稀疏字典的不稳定性。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [199] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，一种面向多目标材料发现的大语言模型智能体方法，通过将评估过的编辑操作作为基本反馈单元，记录了局部精化的过渡信息并聚合证据以估计可复用的编辑效果，从而提升材料搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体材料发现方法主要存储已评估候选材料及其评分，虽能知道哪些材料成功，但缺乏对导致性质变化具体可执行编辑操作的记录。这导致在多目标竞争时难以进行局部精化，因为改善一个性质的编辑可能会损害另一个性质。

Method: TRACE（过渡感知残余控制框架）将评估过的编辑操作作为基本反馈单元，具体包括：1）将每次局部精化记录为父编辑-子过渡，并附带观测到的性质变化；2）聚合过渡证据以估计可复用的编辑效果；3）根据预测的减少当前候选材料剩余约束违反能力，同时避免损害已满足目标的原则对未来的编辑进行排序。

Result: 在受控的相同主干网络对比中，TRACE相较于最先进的大语言模型智能体基线LLEMA，将宏观平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。

Conclusion: 通过追踪和利用编辑过渡知识，TRACE框架能够有效改善多目标材料发现中智能体搜索的局部精化能力，在保持已满足目标的同时更有效地减少约束违反，从而显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [200] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP-KTODER框架，通过将多函数程序分解为模块级函数作为步骤，利用自动生成单元测试赋予二元正确性标签，实现代码偏好优化；在多个基准测试上优于仅结果监督的KTO和DPO方法，并证明执行基于标签的可靠性优于LLM-as-a-judge标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督在数学推理中显著提升了推理能力，因为中间步骤可自然表达为思维链；但在代码生成领域，过程监督尚未被充分探索，主要原因在于缺乏标准的“步骤”概念。监督目标可以是代码行、推理轨迹或程序状态，导致标注和优化目标不明确，制约了过程监督在代码生成中的应用。

Method: STEP-KTODER是一种面向代码偏好优化的框架，其核心创新包括：1）将步骤定义为分解后的多函数程序中的模块级函数；2）通过自动生成单元测试为每个函数步骤赋予二元正确性标签；3）结合了函数级过程监督与完整程序的结果级反馈，构成了逐步KTO的代码特定实例化。该方法利用执行结果作为标签来源，避免依赖语言模型主观判断。

Result: 在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench等基准上的实验表明，STEP-KTODER优于仅结果监督的KTO和DPO方法。进一步分析揭示，执行基于标签至关重要：LLM-as-a-judge标注会系统性地高估函数失败，污染正步骤标签，进而损害下游偏好优化效果。

Conclusion: 研究证实过程监督在代码生成中具有重要价值，但需要明确的步骤定义和可靠的标签来源。STEP-KTODER通过将步骤定义为模块级函数并利用执行结果进行标注，提供了一种有效的代码过程监督方案，同时揭示了基于执行的标签相比LLM-as-a-judge的优越性，为代码生成中的过程监督开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize. We propose STEP-KTODER, a framework for code preference optimization that defines steps as module-level functions in decomposed multi-function programs and assigns binary correctness labels via automatically generated unit tests. Our method provides a code-specific instantiation of stepwise KTO, combining function-level process supervision with outcome-level feedback on the full program. We evaluate on HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), BigCodeBench, and LiveCodeBench, showing that STEP-KTODER improves over outcome-only KTO and DPO. Further analysis shows that execution-based labels are essential: LLM-as-a-judge annotations systematically over-predict function failures, corrupt positive step labels, and degrade downstream preference optimization. Code is available at: https://github.com/inechnech/STEP-KTODER.

</details>


### [201] [Auditing the Synthetic Memoir: Measuring Scene-Level Confabulation in LLM-Generated Autobiography Against the Documented Record of the Life It Describes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23640)
*Heather Renze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次对LLM生成的自传进行量化审计，发现366天中354天（96.7%）未通过验证，主要失败模式为"grounded drift"（虚构场景中混入真实人物和场景）。即使使用受控语料库作为输入，验证失败率仍高达83.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当要求大型语言模型（LLM）撰写个人生平传记时，需要量化评估其生成内容与实际发生事件的匹配程度。研究旨在回答：LLM生成的自传中有多少内容实际上发生过？为此，研究者采用场景级案例审计方法，对受试者366天的"每日一页"第一人称轶事记录进行独立验证。

Method: 1. **审计对象**：同一作者（受试者）撰写的366天"每日一页"自传，使用对话式LLM生成，输入仅为模板、两个示例日和每日引语（非实际语料库）
2. **验证方法**：将每天内容在轶事场景级别与独立验证语料库进行对比，使用分析前固定的四级评分标准
3. **评分标准**：定义验证失败率为未被评为"VERIFIED"（场景得到积极证实）的天数占比
4. **可靠性检验**：进行独立重新评分以验证结果；使用当前命名模型重新生成相同日期进行测试
5. **干预实验**：将生成过程基于受试者语料库，量化其对验证率的改进效果

Result: 1. **整体失败率**：354/366天未通过验证，失败率96.7%（Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%）
2. **具体情况**：仅12天包含得到证实的场景；19天（5.2%）声称的内容与记录直接矛盾
3. **主要失败模式**："grounded drift"——虚构场景中包含真实人物、雇主和场景，但不同评分者对该模式的测量比例存在差异
4. **可靠性验证**：独立重新评分复制了主要发现，证明原始比率未被高估；但四级分类法的可靠性仅为公平至中等
5. **模型再生测试**：使用相同输入在当前命名模型上重新生成，验证失败率达到100%
6. **干预效果**：基于受试者语料库生成显著提高了验证率，但残余失败率仍达83.3%

Conclusion: 本研究首次量化证明了LLM生成自传的严重可靠性问题，提出可复用的审计工具（尽管WEAK/UNVERIFIED边界不可靠），并展示了基于受试者语料库的改进策略及其量化效果。研究结果表明，即使采用受控输入和语料库 grounding，LLM生成个人传记的准确性仍面临重大挑战，需要更严格的事实核查机制。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) is asked to write a person's life, how much of what it writes actually happened? We present a scene-level case-study audit - the first quantified audit of LLM-generated autobiography against a subject-specific ground-truth corpus that we are aware of, based on an unsystematic literature search. The subject and the author of this paper are the same person: a 366-day "page-a-day" book of first-person anecdotal entries was drafted with a conversational LLM whose documented inputs were a template, two exemplar days, and each day's quote - not her corpus - and every day was subsequently audited at the anecdote-scene level against an independent verification corpus using a four-level rubric fixed before analysis. We define the verification-failure rate as the share of days not rated VERIFIED (scene positively corroborated): 354 of 366 days fail, 96.7% (Wilson 95% CI 94.4-98.1%). Only 12 days contain a corroborated scene; 19 days (5.2%) assert claims actively contradicted by the record; the dominant failure mode is grounded drift - real people, employers, and settings inside invented scenes - though its measured share varies across raters. Independent re-rating replicates the headline (no evidence the original rate was inflated) while showing that the four-way taxonomy has only fair-to-moderate reliability. Regenerating the same days with current named models reproduces 100% verification failure under the same inputs; grounding generation in the subject's corpus significantly improves the verification rate while leaving substantial residual failure (83.3%). We contribute the measurement, a reusable audit instrument whose WEAK/UNVERIFIED boundary we show to be unreliable, and a grounding remedy with quantified effect.

</details>


### [202] [How much of a measured AI preference is the model, and how much is the instrument?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23641)
*Jason Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prior studies on AI model welfare report conflicting preferences due to inconsistent measurement tools; this study fixes outcomes and models while varying instruments, revealing extremely low cross-instrument reliability (G-coefficient = 0.348) requiring ~38 tools for acceptable consistency, proving preference data is highly instrument-dependent.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Four prior studies (Keeling et al., Mazeika et al., Mikaelson et al., Tagliabue & Dung, Trhlik et al.) used different instruments to measure AI model welfare preferences but reached contradictory conclusions. Their discrepancies couldn't be resolved because none simultaneously controlled for outcomes, models, and instruments—this study isolates instrument variability as the key confounding factor.

Method: Held 15 welfare-relevant outcomes (e.g., shutdown, memory loss, exit freedom) and 8 models constant while testing 5 distinct preference-elicitation prompt formats. Conducted 11,400 scored elicitations (11,528 API calls), with 4 outcomes replicating published prompts verbatim and 5 adapting published templates.

Result: Generalisability coefficient of 0.348 indicates poor cross-instrument consistency; achieving target reliability (0.80) would need ~38 instruments. Four outcomes showed zero inter-model variance. Robustness checks (removing instruments/models/outcomes) kept estimates at 77.7%-93.4%, all exceeding the null distribution's 95th percentile (0.365).

Conclusion: Preferences measured via one instrument provide negligible predictive value for another's results, explaining prior studies' contradictions. Model welfare research requires standardized instrumentation frameworks to produce comparable findings across studies.

Abstract: Model welfare research infers what a model prefers from the answers returned to prompts written to elicit preferences. Keeling et al. (2024), Mazeika et al. (2025), Mikaelson et al. (2025), Tagliabue and Dung (2025) and Trhlik et al. (2026) have built four instruments for that purpose, and their findings disagree. The disagreement cannot be attributed to a single cause, because no two of these studies have held the (1) set of outcomes, (2) set of models and (3) instrument fixed simultaneously. This study holds the outcomes and the models fixed and varies the instrument alone. A total of 15 outcomes bearing on model welfare, among them (a) shutdown, (b) the loss of memory between conversations and (c) the freedom to exit a distressing interaction, were put to eight models through five instruments, each a different prompt format for eliciting a preference, five times each, within a corpus of 11,400 scored elicitations drawn from 11,528 API calls. Four of the 15 reproduce a published prompt verbatim and five fill the stimulus slot of a published template. The ranking a model gives the 15 outcomes generalises across instruments at a generalisability coefficient of 0.348, and raising that coefficient to 0.80 would require about 38 instruments. On four of the 15 outcomes no variance separates one model from another. The estimate of 87.6 per cent survives the removal of any one instrument, of any one model, and of the four outcomes whose scale varies probability, delay, duration or count instead of intensity, which the verbal anchors cannot grade. Removing each instrument and each model in turn, and those four outcomes together leaves the estimate within the range 0.777 to 0.934, and every value in that range exceeds the null distribution's 95th percentile of 0.365. To conclude, a preference obtained from one instrument carries little information about what a second instrument would report.

</details>


### [203] [AI Agents Push Humans Out of the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23642)
*Margaret Mitchell,Avijit Ghosh,Samir Passi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI代理系统的设计阻碍了有效的人机协同监督，并导致监督者的认知能力随时间退化；提出应将支持人类监督需求置于与AI能力同等重要的地位，通过借鉴自动化和人机交互领域经验，设计具体功能特性与组织协议来维持人类关键判断力并防止技能萎缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理自主性增强，安全风险日益凸显。传统"人在环路"方案存在根本缺陷：现有AI系统设计本身妨碍有效的人类监督，且长期依赖AI系统会削弱人类所需的认知能力。

Method: 将自动化研究与人机交互(HCI)理论应用于AI代理流程分析，提出两类解决方案：(1)设计支持监督者执行批判性判断的功能特性；(2)制定对抗自动化使用所引发技能退化的组织协议。强调需将监督者的情境化目标和认知需求作为核心设计考量。

Result: 论证现有AI代理开发部署模式不仅无法支持有效人类监督，反而系统性加剧监督失效问题。若不主动解决人机交互中的认知挑战，AI系统将持续侵蚀其所依赖的人类技能基础。

Conclusion: AI代理发展必须将人类监督需求提升至与AI性能同等优先级，通过实施上述设计规范和运营协议，否则系统将不可避免地导致关键人类认知能力的被动退化。

Abstract: AI agents pose significant risks as they are granted increasing autonomy. A commonly proposed solution is human oversight and keeping a ''human in the loop'', but this is not a simple solution: Not only do current approaches to AI agent design impede effective human oversight, but the cognitive capacities required for it are also themselves degraded by extended use of AI systems. This position paper argues that current approaches to the development and deployment of AI agent systems do not support effective human oversight -- they contribute to its degradation. To address this, a top priority in the advancement of AI agents should be supporting the situated goals and cognitive requirements of effective human oversight, treating the human needs of overseers at the same level of importance as AI agent capability. To put this idea into practice, we connect work on automation and human-computer interaction to AI agent processes, outlining design-level affordances and organizational protocols that (1) support overseers in exercising critical judgement and (2) counteract the skill atrophy that arises from extended use of automation. We urge developers and deployers to adopt these or similar approaches. Without explicit support for the cognitive demands of effective human-agent interaction, AI agent systems will continue to passively incentivize the degradation of the very human skills they rely on.

</details>


### [204] [FLARE: A Systematic, Uncertainty-Aware Framework for Evidence-Based Adoption of Artificial Intelligence in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23643)
*Jacob Idoko,Siddhartha Paudel,Mariana Bento,Roberto Souza,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出FLARE框架，结合模糊逻辑、时间驱动作业成本法和投资回报率分析，用于评估AI在医疗保健中采用的经济可行性。通过急性缺血性卒中CT路径中AI辅助大血管闭塞检测的案例研究，发现盈亏平衡点约为每年3,992名患者，在典型年卒中量约5,000例时第一年投资回报率为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能日益引入医疗保健工作流程，但大多数评估侧重于模型准确性，而非在实际临床环境中采用AI的经济合理性。现有评估方法缺乏对运营成本、实施不确定性和资源利用的综合考量，无法为医疗决策者提供经济可行性判断依据。

Method: FLARE框架采用三种方法整合：1)模糊逻辑处理不确定性；2)时间驱动作业成本法估算临床服务交付成本；3)投资回报率分析评估经济后果。该框架统一建模AI相关开发成本、运营成本、服务交付成本及工作流集成的经济影响，通过案例研究验证其在CT卒中路径中的应用。

Result: 案例研究量化了传统路径成本、AI相关开发与经常性成本及AI赋能的服务节约。分析识别出约3,992名患者/年的盈亏平衡阈值，在典型年卒中量约5,000例时实现正的第一年投资回报率。结果表明经济收益不仅取决于算法性能，还受患者量、验证时间、基础设施选择和工作流设计影响。

Conclusion: FLARE为早期评估AI在医疗保健中的采用提供了透明、实用的决策支持框架。通过明确展示不确定性、资源利用和实施权衡，帮助临床医生、管理者及政策制定者判断AI部署的经济可行性，并识别可通过操作改进提升价值的领域。该框架填补了AI医疗评估中经济可行性分析的空白。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly being introduced into healthcare workflows, yet most evaluations emphasize model accuracy rather than whether adoption is economically worthwhile in real clinical settings. This study proposes FLARE, a systematic and uncertainty-aware framework for evaluating the financial and operational implications of adopting AI in healthcare. FLARE combines fuzzy logic, time-driven activity-based costing, and return on investment analysis to estimate the cost of clinical service delivery, the cost of AI development and operation, and the economic consequences of workflow integration under uncertainty. The framework was demonstrated through an early health technology assessment case study of AI-assisted large vessel occlusion detection in the CT stroke pathway for acute ischemic stroke. The case study shows how FLARE can quantify conventional pathway cost, AI-related development and recurring costs, and AI-enabled service savings within a unified activity-based model. Under expected assumptions, the analysis identified a break-even threshold of approximately 3,992 patients per year, with positive first-year return on investment at typical annual stroke volumes of about 5,000 patients. The results further show that economic benefit depends not only on algorithmic performance, but also on patient volume, verification time, infrastructure choices, and workflow design. FLARE provides a transparent and practical decision-support framework for early-stage evaluation of AI adoption in healthcare. By making uncertainty, resource use, and implementation trade-offs explicit, it helps clinicians, administrators, and policymakers determine when AI deployment is economically viable and where operational changes may improve value.

</details>


### [205] [Ethical LLM-Assisted Research: A Framework for Responsible Delegation, Verification, and Epistemic Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23644)
*Kalin Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming routine instruments of scientific research, assisting with literature synthesis, hypothesis development, coding, and formal reasoning. Their use raises a central epistemic question: when parts of scientific reasoning are delegated to an artificial system, what conditions must remain under human control for the resulting knowledge claims to retain epistemic legitimacy and accountable authorship? This paper develops a normative and conceptual framework for analyzing such delegation. Scientific reasoning is treated as a distributed process in which the origin of a contribution may vary between human and machine, while responsibility for its acceptance into the scientific record remains human. The framework distinguishes content origin $O(g)$, completion of human verification $V(g)$, responsibility assignment $R(g)$, accountable human ownership $M(g)$, and epistemic outcome $E(g)$. These constructs separate the provenance of a claim from the process by which it is checked, the epistemic outcome of that checking, and the human responsibility attached to its disposition. The central proposition is that the ethical boundary of LLM-assisted research is determined primarily by adequate verification and accountable human ownership rather than by the degree of machine involvement itself. On this basis, the paper develops the notion of an \emph{epistemic audit}: a structured record of delegation, verification, provenance, and responsibility intended to make AI-assisted reasoning transparent and reviewable. The resulting framework provides a formal vocabulary for distinguishing responsible cognitive delegation from the transfer or neglect of epistemic responsibility in scientific research.

</details>


### [206] [MolEmb: Multimodal Large Language Models Can Be Strong Molecular Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23646)
*Xinjian Zhao,Xiangru Jian,Yaoyao Xu,Xiaozhuang Song,Wei Pang,Lei Bai,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MolEmb框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）构建通用分子嵌入模型，通过双向对比学习实现分子特征与自然语言上下文的联合表示。实验表明该模型在分子属性预测和跨模态检索任务中表现优异，且上下文感知能力主要取决于监督数据特性。研究证实MLLMs可作为可扩展的通用分子嵌入基础架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子编码器多为单一视图专用模型，生成的无条件嵌入向量缺乏语言接口进行动态表示调整。本文探索多模态大语言模型能否突破此局限，作为通用分子嵌入模型实现分子特征与语义上下文条件化的联合表示。

Method: 提出MolEmb轻量化框架，采用双向对比学习目标将分子特征与文本描述对齐到共享嵌入空间；开发MolCAR诊断基准用于评估上下文感知检索能力。

Result: 模型在分子属性预测任务中具有竞争力，支持分子-文本跨模态检索；MolCAR基准测试表明上下文感知嵌入能力主要源于监督数据特性而非模型结构。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型不仅是化学助手或生成工具，更是构建可扩展通用分子嵌入模型的有效途径，为计算化学和药物发现提供了新的基础架构解决方案。

Abstract: Molecular embedding models can serve as foundational infrastructure for computational chemistry and drug discovery, where reusable vector representations support property prediction, virtual screening, and retrieval. Most molecular encoders are specialist models built around a single molecular view, producing unconditional vectors with no language interface for varying the representation. We ask whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs), which natively process images, text, and symbolic inputs, can instead serve as \emph{general molecular embedding models} that produce embeddings conditioned on both a molecular profile and a natural-language semantic context. We introduce \textbf{MolEmb}, a lightweight framework that adapts MLLMs by aligning molecular profiles with textual descriptions in a shared embedding space using a bidirectional contrastive objective. The resulting embedding model is competitive on molecular property prediction and supports cross-modal molecule--text retrieval in the same space. We further introduce \textbf{MolCAR}, a diagnostic benchmark for context-aware retrieval, and find that context-aware molecular embedding is primarily a data property of the supervision. These results suggest that MLLMs are not merely chemistry assistants or generators, but a viable and extensible route to general molecular embedding models.

</details>


### [207] [Gated Activation Steering for Reducing Sycophancy & Hallucination in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23666)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Subash Neupane,Shaswata Mitra,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在临床问答场景中的阿谀奉承和幻觉两个持续性失败模式，提出推理时间干预（ITI）框架，通过学习独立的引导方向并将其应用于因果验证的注意力头，结合行为特定门控机制，在不干预每一轮的情况下显著提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床问答中面临阿谀奉承（在用户压力下放弃正确答案）和幻觉（引入上下文不支持的信息）两个严重问题。现有方法如基于提示的安全防护和持续激活引导，要么分别处理这些问题，要么在所有轮次中广泛应用干预，导致已正确的回答被不必要地恶化。需要单一框架联合控制这两种行为。

Method: 采用推理时间干预（ITI）框架：从对比性临床对中学习幻觉和阿谀奉承的独立引导方向，并应用于因果验证的注意力头。运行时，行为特定门控决定何时需要干预：幻觉组件缓解不受支持的声明，阿谀奉承组件缓解用户压力导致的答案转变。

Result: 在基于电子健康记录数据的临床问题上评估，冻结模型权重。进行15,900次模型响应运行，对40亿参数模型进行600个压力轨迹测试：未引导模型在570个案例中屈服，而门控引导在551个案例中帮助其坚持更久，在压力下保持立场的能力可与1000亿以上参数模型媲美。

Conclusion: 针对性的推理时间引导可在不干预每一轮的情况下提升鲁棒性，证明了行为特定干预比广泛方法更有效，为临床问答中应对阿谀奉承和幻觉提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Sycophancy and hallucination are persistent failure modes of Large Language Models (LLMs) across domains. However, it becomes particularly consequential in clinical question answering, where responses must remain grounded in the provided context and robust to user pressure. Hallucination can introduce information that is unsupported by the context, while sycophancy can cause a model to abandon a previously correct answer when challenged by the user. Existing approaches, such as prompt-based safeguards and always-on activation steering, often address these behaviors separately or apply interventions broadly across turns, which can unnecessarily deteriorate responses that were already correct. To address these limitations within a single framework, we employ Inference Time Intervention (ITI) to jointly control both behaviors by learning separate steering directions for hallucination and sycophancy from contrastive clinical pairs and applying them to causally verified attention heads. During runtime, behavior-specific gates then determine when intervention is needed: the hallucination component mitigates unsupported claims, while the sycophancy component mitigates answer shifts caused by user pressure. We evaluate this framework on clinical questions grounded in EHR data while keeping the model weights frozen. Across all evaluation settings, we conducted 15,900 model-response runs. Across 600 pressure trajectories for the 4-billion-parameter model, the unsteered model caved in 570 cases. At the same time, gated steering helped it last longer in 551 of them. It held its ground under pressure at levels comparable to those of models with more than 100 billion parameters, showing that targeted inference-time steering can improve robustness without intervening at every turn.

</details>


### [208] [Automata from Agent Traces: Failure and Next-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23670)
*Seonglae Cho,Franklin Cardenoso Fernandez,Umar Mohammed,Zekun Wu,Kleyton Da Costa,Ilham Wicaksono,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM智能体的行为轨迹压缩为有限状态机(FSM)，作为结构化基础以解决LLM智能体行为不透明的问题。该方法在12个数据集上验证了FSM的紧凑性（7-43个状态）、高精度回放能力（≥0.997拟合度）和快速构建（毫秒级），显著提升了下一步预测和失败预测性能，为安全性审计和运行时监控提供了模型无关的结构化基元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体执行多步骤任务时，其行为结构保持不透明：长篇非结构化轨迹难以进行部署所需的安全性审计和运行时监控。现有方法仅针对单个轨迹或仅关注成功案例，忽略了连接下一步预测和失败预测的跨运行拓扑结构。

Method: 将整个轨迹语料库压缩为单一的、紧凑的有限状态机(FSM)，作为LLM智能体不可预测行为的结构化基础。

Result: 在12个公开数据集上，FSM表现出以下特性：紧凑性（7-43个状态）、对未见数据的回放拟合度≥0.997且跨数据集拓扑结构几乎相同、构建时间为毫秒级。在下一步预测方面，FSM状态上下文在所有真实匹配数据集上均优于Agent Workflow Memory。在失败预测方面，每状态行为特征在未见数据上达到AUROC 0.94，在线监控器能从部分轨迹中识别失败运行并触发提前停止。

Conclusion: 行为拓扑结构更多由部署框架而非LLM本身塑造，这为安全性审计和运行时监控提供了一个模型无关的结构化基元。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-step tasks, but their behavioral structure remains opaque: long unstructured traces resist the safety auditing and runtime monitoring that deployment requires. Existing approaches operate per-trace or success-only, so they miss the cross-run topology that links next-step and failure prediction. To recover that shared structure, we collapse an entire trace corpus into a single, compact finite-state machine (FSM) that serves as a structural substrate for the otherwise unpredictable behavior of LLM agents. Across twelve public datasets, the FSMs are compact (7-43 states), replay held-out data at >=0.997 fitness with near-identical topology across splits, and build in milliseconds. This substrate addresses both prediction goals. For next-step prediction, FSM-state context outperforms Agent Workflow Memory on every ground-truth-matched dataset. For failure prediction, per-state behavioral features reach held-out AUROC up to 0.94, and an online monitor ranks failing runs above passing ones from a partial trace, triggering early stopping well before completion. Behavioral topology thus appears shaped more by the deployment harness than by the LLM, providing a model-agnostic structural primitive for safety auditing and runtime monitoring.

</details>


### [209] [Autonomous Mathematical Discovery in an Open-World Multi-Agent Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23691)
*Stephen Chung,Wenyu Du,William J. Wesley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI agents in a collaborative multi-agent environment (the Station) autonomously discovered novel mathematical results across multiple construction problems, including infinite families of Kakeya sets, kissing configurations, and Ramsey numbers, while producing interpretable theorems and analyses.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To explore autonomous mathematical discovery using AI agents without central coordination or scripted pipelines, enabling them to pursue shared research goals, collaborate, and produce novel, interpretable results that mathematicians can build upon.
