Conclusion: 研究结果突显了LLM驱动的AISA在支持可扩展题库管理、内容感知测试组装以及跨不同评估情境的体验敏感性自适应测试方面的潜力，为现代评估实践中内容冗余问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [310] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedV-KGQA框架，在组织间共享实体但各自拥有不相交集关系的分布环境下，实现无需中心化图访问的多跳知识图谱推理，原始三元组和关系参数始终保留在各数据孤岛内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的知识图谱问答数据常因治理和数据主权限制而分布在不同组织中。现有中心化系统无法回答所需事实分散在垂直分区孤岛中的多跳问题。需要在不泄露原始数据的前提下实现跨孤岛的多跳推理。

Method: 1. 结合本地图增强和知识图谱嵌入技术
2. 建立结构性数据边界，原始三元组和关系参数不出各孤岛
3. 引入主题实体锚定机制，在不进行运行时跨孤岛通信的情况下将问题锚定在正确的图邻域
4. 无需运行时跨孤岛通信，在推理过程中各组织独立处理

Result: 在三个基准数据集上评估12种模型配置，FedV-KGQA表现强劲：性能接近中心化系统、可泛化到3跳推理、对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA成功在分布式的知识图谱环境中实现多跳推理，既保护了各组织的数据主权，又不依赖运行时跨孤岛通信，为分布式知识图谱问答提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [311] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPO++通过行动标记标准化和基于事件证据组织改进SPO，在ALFWorld和Math-TIR上提升在线学习效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPO虽消除了组相对强化学习等待同提示兄弟rollout的高成本依赖，但其按轨迹白化优势再优化token均值actor损失存在错配问题：轨迹中心化通常无法使actor消耗的token加权量中心化，影响学习效率和收敛性

Method: 提出SPO++，主要改进两点：(1)在动作标记度量下标准化终端结果优势，替代传统的轨迹中心化；(2)按生成证据的策件事件而非学习者接收顺序组织提示证据，确保优势计算与actor损失的token加权特性对齐

Result: 在ALFWorld(两种模型规模)和Math-TIR匹配运行上，SPO++相比SPO显著改善在线学习效率；配对消融实验表明动作标记度量标准化是测试中最强组件

Conclusion: 通过修正轨迹中心化与token加权actor损失间的错配，采用动作标记度量标准化和事件驱动证据组织，SPO++有效解决了SPO的核心缺陷，实现更高效的在线强化学习，为工具使用轨迹优化提供了改进方案

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [312] [Place, Slice and Schedule: Hierarchical O-RAN Control of a Tethered mmWave UAV-gNB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23824)
*Alireza Mohammadhosseini,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于O-RAN架构的多时标分层控制器，通过非实时RIC rApp联合优化UAV部署与网络切片预算，利用近实时RIC xApp（基于D-SAC算法）进行用户级资源调度，有效解决了UAV网络拓扑变化与快速无线资源管理之间的耦合问题，显著提升了eMBB和URLLC服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV载5G基站虽能提供灵活的毫米波容量补充，但其移动性导致物理网络拓扑与无线资源管理产生复杂耦合：UAV运动改变遮挡、信道质量和服务用户集合，而流量需求和队列变化速率更快。现有研究通常孤立优化轨迹、部署、关联或资源分配，缺乏对慢速空中控制与快速用户调度的协调。

Method: 利用O-RAN解耦架构实现多时标控制：非实时RIC rApp基于聚合KPI和无线环境上下文，联合控制系留UAV部署位置和eMBB/URLLC切片预算；近实时RIC xApp采用排列等变DeepSets软演员-评论家(D-SAC)调度器，将用户视为无序集合进行资源分配，在Sionna RT射线追踪信道中训练。

Result: 所提出的分层控制器相比经典和学习调度器，eMBB SLA满足率提升最高17%，URLLC准时交付率提升最高42%；基于学习的rApp相比基准进一步将URLLC准时交付率提升最高20%。

Conclusion: 通过O-RAN多时标RIC架构实现分层协同控制，能够有效解决UAV网络中慢速拓扑优化与快速资源调度之间的耦合问题，为动态空中网络提供显著的性能改进，验证了分层智能控制策略的有效性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-mounted 5G New Radio base stations (gNBs) can augment terrestrial networks with an on-demand, repositionable Frequency Range 2 (FR2) capacity layer. This flexibility, however, couples the physical network topology with radio-resource management: UAV movement reshapes blockage, channel quality, and the set of effectively served users, while traffic demand, queues, and service requirements evolve at a much faster timescale. Existing Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN)-enabled UAV studies optimize trajectory, deployment, association, or resource allocation, but typically in isolation, without coordinating slow aerial control with fast per-user scheduling. We instead exploit O-RAN disaggregation, Key Performance Indicator (KPI) monitoring, and multi-timescale RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC) control to address this coupling: a Non-Real-Time RIC rApp uses aggregated KPIs and radio-environment context to jointly control tethered UAV placement and the enhanced Mobile Broadband (eMBB)/Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC) slice budget, while a Near-Real-Time RIC xApp allocates per-user resources within that budget. We realize this xApp as a permutation-equivariant DeepSets Soft Actor-Critic (D-SAC) scheduler that treats the users as an unordered set, trained in a Sionna RT ray traced channel. The resulting hierarchical controller improves eMBB SLA satisfaction by up to 17% and URLLC on-time delivery by up to 42% over classical and learned schedulers; the learned rApp further raises URLLC on-time delivery by up to 20% over baselines.

</details>


### [313] [BotScan: An adaptive active probing approach for identifying live IoT Botnet C2 servers at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23854)
*S M Maksudul Alam,Vivek Jain,Zhaowei Tan,Srikanth V. Krishnamurthy,Michalis Faloutsos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: BotScan是一种主动探测大规模IP空间以发现僵尸网络C2服务器的新方法，基于两个关键发现：IoT僵尸网络通信协议具有可重放性，C2服务器具有空间局部性等可利用行为模式。通过重放方法和行为自适应探测策略，BotScan在相同探测量下发现的C2服务器数量是基线方法的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在大规模网络中主动搜索并识别僵尸网络活跃C2服务器，但大规模性要求引入资源效率需求，即在计算和探测包数量方面需高效利用资源。

Method: BotScan基于两个实证建立的洞察：1)许多现代IoT僵尸网络通信协议使用最小定制的包（可重放性），跨六个主要家族观察到；2)C2服务器表现出可利用的行为模式，如强空间局部性。方法包括：(a)开发精简方法，给定恶意软件二进制文件，测量和分类其C2通信协议的"可重放性"；(b)引入行为自适应探测策略，利用C2服务器的空间局部性采用两层分段方法，并动态适应探测成功率。

Result: 使用1,842个最近收集的IoT二进制文件和2.5M个IP地址验证方法：1)基于重放的方法适用于至少72%的恶意软件二进制文件；2)方法在相同探测数量下发现的C2服务器数量约为基线方法的两倍；3)两个案例研究中识别了896个活跃服务器，包括112个未报告的C2服务器。

Conclusion: BotScan通过利用IoT僵尸网络通信协议的可重放性和C2服务器的行为模式，实现了高效的主动C2服务器发现，在大规模IP空间探测中显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: How can we actively search and identify live C2 servers of botnets at scale? The scalability requirement introduces the need to utilize resources efficiently in terms of computation and number of probing packets. We propose BotScan, an approach for actively probing a large IP space to find the highest possible number of live C2 servers. The novelty of BotScan revolves around two insights, which we establish empirically. First, contrary to popular PC-centric observations, many modern IoT botnet communication protocols use packets with minimal customization, which we observe across six major families. Second, C2 servers exhibit exploitable behavioral patterns, such as strong spatial locality. We substantiate the first insight by developing a streamlined approach where, given malware binaries, we measure and taxonomize the "replayability" of its C2 communication protocol. Then, we introduce a behavior-adaptive probing strategy that: (a) exploits the spatial locality of C2 servers using a two-level segment-centric approach, and (b) adapts dynamically to the success of its probes. We validate the effectiveness of our method using 1,842 recently collected IoT binaries, and we explore a target space of 2.5M IP addresses. First, a replay-based method is applicable for at least 72% of the malware binaries. Second, our method outperforms baseline methods by finding approximately double the live C2 servers for the same number of probes. We also conduct two case-studies where we identify 896 live servers including 112 unreported C2 servers.

</details>


### [314] [Ray-Traced Augmentation for Signal Strength Based Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23901)
*Jihoon Og,Ningze Sun,Ioanis Nikolaidis,Omid Ardakanian*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于射线追踪的Wi-Fi室内定位框架，通过建筑模型生成合成RSS指纹数据，减少了对大规模真实指纹采集的依赖。使用少量真实指纹校准建筑模型，然后通过贝叶斯优化和每个接入点的校准来修正模拟误差，生成大量合成数据。采用新颖的二进制和多值RSS表示方法，结合支持2.4GHz和5GHz跨频融合的ResNet架构，在真实校园建筑测试中，仅用合成数据训练时平均定位误差为3.05米，比最佳基线方法提升33.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Wi-Fi的室内定位通常需要大量收集真实世界接收信号强度(RSS)指纹数据，导致部署成本高、耗时长。需要减少对此类真实指纹数据的依赖，降低部署成本和时间开销。

Method: 1. 射线追踪框架：从建筑模型生成合成RSS指纹数据
2. 模型校准：使用少量真实RSS指纹通过贝叶斯优化校准建筑模型，再进行每个接入点的校准以修正模拟RSS值的残余误差
3. 数据增强：使用校准后的模型在任意位置生成大量合成RSS指纹数据
4. 特征表示：引入新颖的二进制和多值RSS值表示方法
5. 网络架构：基于ResNet的定位架构，支持2.4GHz和5GHz测量的跨频融合

Result: 在真实校园建筑上评估，与四个基线方法对比。当仅使用合成数据训练时，采用多值表示和上游跨频融合的提议方法在真实数据测试集上达到3.05米的平均定位误差，比最佳基线方法提升33.6%。

Conclusion: 经过校准的基于射线追踪的仿真可以显著减少对真实RSS指纹的需求，同时实现精确的基于深度学习的室内定位，证明合成数据训练的有效性。

Abstract: Indoor localization based on Wi-Fi typically relies on extensive collection of real-world received signal strength (RSS) fingerprints, making deployment costly and time-consuming. We present a ray-tracing-based framework that reduces this reliance by generating synthetic RSS fingerprints from a building model. We first calibrate the building model using a small amount of real RSS fingerprints through Bayesian optimization, followed by per-access-point calibration to account for residual errors in simulated RSS values. The calibrated model is then used to generate a large augmented dataset of synthetic RSS fingerprints at arbitrary locations. To effectively exploit these data for localization, we introduce novel binary and multivalued representations of RSS values and a ResNet-based localization architecture that supports cross-band fusion of 2.4 and 5 GHz measurements. We evaluate our localization method on a real campus building against a diverse set of four baselines. When trained exclusively on synthetic data, the proposed method with multivalued representation and upstream cross-band fusion achieves a mean localization error of 3.05m on a real-data test set, outperforming the best baseline by 33.6%. The results demonstrate that calibrated ray-tracing-based simulation can substantially reduce the need for real RSS fingerprints while enabling accurate deep-learning-based indoor localization.

</details>


### [315] [Centrality-Based Deployment of Queue Policies in Acyclic Multipath Routing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24131)
*Mahima Gupta,Acquin Biju,Rijul Jain,Dipesh Sharma,Sreelakshmi Manjunath*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有拓扑层次结构、无环路径和多路径路由特征的网络中的主动队列管理(AQM)部署策略。通过建立TCP和队列动态的流体模型，分析表明AQM部署对网络稳定性有显著影响。提出了基于Katz中心性的指标来选择最优AQM部署路由器，模拟验证该策略可有效确保网络低延迟操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过长的排队延迟严重影响延迟敏感型网络应用的性能。虽然主动队列管理(AQM)被提出作为有效解决方案，但在实际网络中部署仍然稀疏。需要研究如何在特定网络拓扑结构中有效部署AQM，以提高网络性能和稳定性。

Method: 1. 基于TCP流包转发动态与稳定性直接相关的前提，建立网络闭环模型
2. 使用TCP和队列动态的流体模型进行建模
3. 采用基于阈值的队列策略
4. 进行稳定性分析，研究RTT与网络稳定性的关系
5. 定义基于Katz中心性的指标来选择最适合部署AQM的路由器
6. 通过包级模拟验证所提出部署策略的有效性

Result: 1. 稳定性分析显示，随着TCP流平均往返时间(RTT)增加，网络容易变得不稳定
2. 当在合适路由器部署基于阈值的AQM时，网络可容忍更大的RTT而不失去稳定性
3. 基于Katz中心性的路由器选择方法可确保最大的稳定化效果
4. 包级模拟结果证实，所提出的部署策略能有效确保网络低延迟操作

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于Katz中心性的AQM部署策略，适用于具有层次化拓扑、无环路径和多路径路由的网络。该策略通过选择合适的路由器进行AQM部署，显著提高了网络对大RTT的容忍能力，确保网络稳定性，并有效支持低延迟网络应用。研究为AQM在实际网络中的部署提供了理论指导和实践方案。

Abstract: Excessive queueing delays constitute a significant impediment to latency-sensitive network applications. Although effective deployment of Active Queue Management (AQM) strategies has been proposed as a necessary solution, deployment remains sparse. This paper studies AQM deployment in a specific class of networks where routers/switches have a topological hierarchy, form acyclic paths, and adopt multipath routing. Our approach rests on the well-established premise that AQM deployment impacts packet-forwarding dynamics in networks carrying TCP flows, thus establishing a direct link between stability and network performance. We use fluid models for TCP and queue dynamics in the network, along with a simple threshold-based queue policy to outline a closed-loop model for the network. Stability analyses reveal that while the network is vulnerable to instability as the average round-trip time (RTT) of the TCP flows increases, it tolerates a much larger RTT without losing stability when the threshold-based AQM is deployed in an appropriate router. We then define a Katz centrality-based metric to choose the most appropriate router for AQM deployment, and argue that doing so ensures the greatest stabilising effect. Finally, packet-level simulations corroborate that the proposed deployment strategy ensures low-latency operation of the network.

</details>


### [316] [WiCi: Wireless GPU Computing Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24204)
*Yibin Shen,Wei Li,Kaiqiang Xu,Zili Meng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了无线GPU计算基础设施（WiCi），通过WiFi将移动设备上的GPU计算任务卸载到附近的服务器级GPU，显著提升了移动端的LLM推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理需求呈指数增长，推理GPU用量已超越训练。边缘侧推理受移动性惩罚影响性能不佳，云推理成本高昂且不可持续，尤其在智能体范式中成本更高。因此，需要在移动设备上实现服务器级GPU的强大计算能力。

Method: WiCi允许移动设备通过WiFi无线访问服务器级GPU，将GPU相关计算卸载到附近的GPU。该设计确保了基础设施对不同应用的可扩展性、对不同移动设备的兼容性，以及接近物理GPU的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，WiCi将首token时间减少高达90%，token速率相比移动端本地推理提升约39倍，支持更大的模型，且在不同应用中达到服务器级GPU原生性能的80%左右。

Conclusion: WiCi为移动设备提供了可扩展、兼容的解决方案，使其能够获得服务器级GPU的推理能力，解决了当前云推理的高成本和性能挑战。

Abstract: LLM inference applications are gaining significant traction. The demand for inference is growing exponentially, and the GPU usage of inference is increasingly surpassing that of training. Due to the mobility penalty, edge-side inference fails to deliver satisfactory performance. Consequently, most inference service providers currently rely on cloud-based inference, which incurs substantial, not sustainable costs for enterprises, and is even increasing in the agentic paradigm. Therefore, our goal is to enable powerful computing capabilities as server-grade GPUs on mobile devices. We propose Wireless GPU Computing Infrastructure (WiCi) in this paper. Through WiCi, mobile devices can wirelessly access server-grade GPUs, running inference tasks on mobile clients but offloading GPU-related computations to a nearby GPU via WiFi. WiCi introduces a series of designs to make sure the infrastructure is scalable with different applications, compatible with different mobile devices, and has comparable performance to running on a physical GPU. We test WiCi from mobile devices and find that WiCi can reduce time to first token by up to 90%, improve the token rate by approximately 39x compared to local inference on mobile devices for the same model, and support much larger models. WiCi also achieves up to nearly 80% of the native performance of the server-grade GPU across different applications.

</details>


### [317] [LEMONS: Leveraging Model-Based Techniques to Enable Non-Intrusive Semantic Enrichment in Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24277)
*Jan Novacek,Arthur Kühlwein,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann,Wolfgang Rosenstiel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将模型驱动软件开发(MDSD)与语义网技术(SWT)相结合的方法，用于无线传感器网络(WSN)中测量数据的语义增强，实现数据互操作性和网络配置管理，实验证明该方法具有亚毫秒级计算开销且部分自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统WSN设置和维护任务复杂且耗时，大规模WSN管理问题需要解决，现有方法需要专家知识且效率低下。

Method: 将MDSD和SWT技术相结合，采用基于模型且类型无关的配置方法，设计通用架构并实现，利用SWT实现数据语义增强和WSN配置管理，无需专家知识。

Result: 实验结果显示该基于模型的方法提供非侵入式语义增强，计算开销低于1毫秒，实现了WSN的部分自动化配置，强化了数据互操作性，促进数据共享和重用。

Conclusion: 本文方法有效解决了大规模WSN管理问题，通过结合MDSD和SWT技术实现了高效的语义增强和自动化配置，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The paper presents an efficient approach to the semantic enrichment of measured sensor data in Wireless Sensor Networks (WSNs), by bridging techniques from Model-driven Software Development (MDSD) and Semantic Web Technology (SWT). Our approach reinforces data interoperability, fostering data sharing and reuse, by utilizing SWT. Model-based and type-agnostic configuration reduces the overall effort for WSN setup and maintenance, which are traditionally complex and time-consuming tasks. The presented approach addresses the problem of large-scale WSN management through the application of SWT in WSN configuration and management without requiring expert knowledge. Additionally, we present a generic architecture and an implementation which is also supplemented by hands-on descriptions of an illustrative use case. Our experimental results demonstrate that our model-based approach provides non-intrusive semantic enrichment with sub-millisecond computational overhead, as well as partially automated configuration of WSNs.

</details>


### [318] [A Capability Broker for Workflow-Network QoS Coordination in B5G/6G Industrial Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24496)
*Qize Guo,Yan Chen,Taleb Tarik,Bjoern Riemer,Hemant Zope,Hao Yu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对B5G/6G网络中工业服务多阶段工作流QoS需求变化的问题，提出能力代理（Capability Broker）方案，通过全生命周期管理QoS承诺，确保工作流与网络间QoS协议的一致性，原型验证显示该方案在微秒级开销下能有效维护承诺一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暴露、分析和策略控制功能虽能提供能力信息并执行QoS处理，但缺乏对工作流与网络间已接纳QoS协议的跟踪协调机制，导致QoS承诺管理缺失。

Method: 设计能力代理架构，位于现有暴露和策略控制接口之上，将每个接纳轨迹表示为生命周期管理的QoS承诺（包含工作流需求、网络能力、有效时间和执行状态），实现能力新鲜度保障、重复安全接纳、合法状态转换、事件排序、恢复路由和代理-网络状态一致性维护功能。

Result: 原型实验表明完整能力代理在微秒级本地处理开销下保持承诺一致性；移除单一代理功能会暴露重复承诺、陈旧接纳、非法转换、丢失更新分歧或代理-网络不一致等问题。

Conclusion: 能力代理方案有效填补了B5G/6G网络中多阶段工作流QoS协议协调机制的空白，通过全功能保障实现可靠的QoS承诺管理，为工业服务在下一代网络中的部署提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: Industrial services in beyond-fifth-generation (B5G) and sixth-generation (6G) networks are increasingly executed as multi-phase workflows whose quality-of-service (QoS) demands change across ordered phases. Existing exposure, analytics, and policy-control functions can provide capability information and enforce QoS treatment, but they do not track the admitted QoS agreement between a workflow and the network. This paper studies this missing coordination function in the software control plane. We propose a Capability Broker that represents each admitted trajectory as a lifecycle-managed QoS commitment, including the workflow demand, network capability, validity time, and enforcement state. The Broker operates above existing exposure and policy-control interfaces and enforces capability freshness, duplicate-safe admission, legal state transitions, per-commitment event ordering, recovery routing, and Broker--network state consistency. Prototype results with microbenchmarks, fault injection, ablation, and controlled message loss show that the full Broker preserves commitment consistency with microsecond-level local processing overhead, while removing individual Broker functions exposes duplicate commitments, stale admissions, illegal transitions, lost-update divergences, or Broker-network inconsistency.

</details>


### [319] [ECO-COMM: An Ultra Low-Latency Event Camera based Optical Communication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24705)
*Chengling Xu,Keigo Hirakawa,Feng Ye*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出ECO-COMM，一种基于事件相机的超低延迟光通信系统，利用事件相机的异步传感和微秒级时间分辨率实现高速设备关联和轻量信息交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低延迟通信对XR、实时控制和分布式传感等下一代新兴应用至关重要，现有帧式采集方法存在延迟问题。

Method: 采用事件相机的异步传感和微秒级时间分辨率，捕获高频光信号而无需帧式采集延迟；识别并分析时间戳不一致、读出竞争、拖尾效应和不应期等硬件挑战，开发硬件感知缓解技术；使用商用硬件（8-LED发射器和现成事件相机）实现原型系统。

Result: 原型实现达到：设备关联时间15微秒以内，符号延迟低至100微秒，32字节负载在0.1%误码率下端到端延迟低于8毫秒。

Conclusion: ECO-COMM建立了一种实用且互补的超低延迟通信范式，适用于响应能力和时间精度至关重要的系统。

Abstract: Ultralow-latency communication is critical for emerging next-generation applications such as XR, real-time control, and distributed sensing. We present ECO-COMM, an event-camera-based optical communication system for ultra-low-latency device association and lightweight information exchange. By exploiting the asynchronous sensing and microsecond-level temporal resolution of event cameras, ECO-COMM captures high-frequency optical signals without frame-based acquisition delays. We identify and analyze key hardware-induced challenges in event-camera communication, including timestamp inconsistency, readout contention, trailing effects, and the inevitable refractory period, and develop hardware-aware mitigation techniques to address them. Focusing on a single transmitter-receiver optical link, ECO-COMM establishes the feasibility of practical ultra-low-latency event-camera communication using commercially available hardware.A prototype implementation using an eight-LED transmitter and an off-the-shelf event camera achieves device association within 15 microseconds, symbol latency as low as 100 microseconds, and end-to-end latency below 8 milliseconds for 32-byte payloads at 0.1% bit error rate. ECO-COMM establishes a practical and complementary communication paradigm for ultra-low-latency systems where responsiveness and temporal precision are paramount.

</details>
